- 2026-07-05: DataTable — sort-priority number in column headers. When two or more sorts are stacked, a sorted column's header arrow carries its 1-based position in the priority list (`↑1`, `↓2`, …, from `column.getSortIndex()` in [data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)), so "sorted by Status, then Budget" reads directly off the headers instead of only inside the Sorting editor ; a lone sort shows just the arrow. Verified via the `table-sorting-active` screenshot story ; `tsc` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-05: DataTable — moved the list controls out of the table into the toolbar, with PanelHeader-style overflow collapse. The table-config popover (sliders icon floating in the table header) is gone ; filters, a standalone Sorting editor (extracted from the config menu), and a Columns editor now sit in a new [`TableTools`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/table-tools.tsx) cluster in the `FilterBar`'s new `tools` slot — right-aligned, left of the primary CTA (search stays left ; the CTA never collapses). Layout state (order / visibility / widths / sorting + localStorage persistence) lifted out of `DataTable` into [`useDataTableLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/use-data-table-layout.ts) so the toolbar and table share it ; the sorting / columns panels extracted to [panels.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/panels.tsx). When the toolbar row gets tight the controls collapse right-to-left into one sliders trigger opening a drill-in popover (`FilterBar` measures the row via ResizeObserver and exposes the budget through `useFilterBarToolsBudget` ; fit math shared with `PanelHeader` in [overflow.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/overflow.ts)) ; on mobile everything collapses into a full-screen dialog of labelled sections (reusing `FilterFieldControl`, the plain filter inputs exported from [filter-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx)). All eight list surfaces migrated (projects, industries, users, organizations, rbac, webhooks, deliveries, dev fields gallery) ; `DataTable` dropped its `chrome` prop (nothing passed it) and its labels shrank to row/error strings ; new `table.tools` i18n key (en + fr) ; screenshot stories reworked (`table-toolbar`, `table-tools-sorting`, `table-tools-columns`, `table-tools-collapsed`) and all four states visually verified. `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint`, `check:i18n` green.

- 2026-07-04: Global search — dropped the nav-drawer trigger. Now that the AppBar carries an always-visible search button, the search-field-shaped trigger at the top of the primary-nav drawer was redundant ; removed it from [primary-nav-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/primary-nav-menu.tsx) and deleted the now-unused `global-search-trigger.tsx` (+ its barrel export). The `useIsApplePlatform` hook stays (still used by the AppBar icon button). `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-05: Panels — one standard right-side panel header. Four surfaces each rolled their own drawer header (form detail panels used `PanelHeaderBar` = left collapse + fullpage icons with the title buried in the content ; Apps / Notifications used a `p-4` title block + the Sheet's built-in close X ; the User Menu had a banner/avatar header + a bottom logout footer). New shared [`PanelHeader`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/panel-header.tsx) standardizes them : a fixed 57px bar (aligned to the appbar seam) with optional **left controls** (a `back` arrow, or `window` mode = reduce + open-full-page), a required **title** with an optional **subtitle** stacked below (no height impact), and right-aligned **icon actions** that overflow into a "…" menu based on the **measured panel width** (`ResizeObserver`, capped at `maxVisibleActions`, default 3), with a **close button always pinned right-most**. Migrated all six detail panels ([projects](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/projects-list.tsx>), [industries](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>), [organizations](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/organizations/organizations-list.tsx>), [users](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/users-list.tsx>), [roles](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/rbac/roles-manager.tsx>), [webhooks](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/webhooks-manager.tsx>) — which now show a visible title + a right-most close X, with the open-full-page control on the left ; the old left collapse icon is dropped since the close now covers it) plus the three AppBar drawers ([app-launcher](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-launcher.tsx), [notifications-bell](monark/app/services/web/src/components/notifications-bell.tsx) — mark-all-read is now a header action and the filter tabs a sub-bar, [user-menu](monark/app/services/web/src/components/user-menu.tsx) — the banner/avatar moved into content and logout out of the footer into content flow, header shows name + email). Each drawer drops the Sheet's built-in close (`hideClose`) since `PanelHeader` owns it. Deleted `PanelHeaderBar` ; new shared `common.close` / `common.more` i18n (en + fr) ; a `panel-header` screenshot story exercises every mode + the overflow. Web `tsc` + `eslint` green, 222 tests pass.

- 2026-07-04: DataTable — removed inline cell editing and the primary column's hover open-panel icon. Clicking the primary label now always opens the detail panel (link when `href` is set, button when only `onSelect` is given) ; edits go through the panel's form. Dropped `CellEdit`, the `edit` config on both column defs, the `EditableCell` component, and the `labels.openPanel` string from [data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) + [types.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/types.ts) ; removed the `edit` pass-through + `inlineEditable` metadata from the fields toolkit ([field-column.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-column.ts), [registry.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/registry.tsx)) ; dropped the now-orphaned inline-rename mutations from [projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/projects-list.tsx>) and [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>) and the `table.openPanel` i18n key (en + fr) across all list screens.

- 2026-07-04: Global search — refinements. (1) The "Searching…" row now only appears once a fetch has run past 400ms _and_ there's nothing to show yet (new `useDelayed` gate in [global-search-dialog.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/global-search-dialog.tsx)), so fast responses never flash it ; projects keep their previous rows on screen during a refetch (keepPreviousData), so they never show it at all. (2) Removed the calendar's now-redundant search : deleted `calendar-search.tsx`, its toolbar Search button, and the leftover `isSearchOpen` state from [calendar-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-shell.tsx>) (its ⌘K was already handed to the global palette last change) ; dropped the dead `calendar.search` i18n block in en + fr. (3) Added an always-visible AppBar trigger : a ghost magnifier icon button with a hover tooltip showing the ⌘/Ctrl+K shortcut ([global-search-icon-button.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/global-search-icon-button.tsx)), placed left of the notifications bell, with the "Monark Apps" launcher moved to the right-most of the icon cluster ([app-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-bar.tsx)). Platform-glyph detection factored into a shared `useIsApplePlatform` hook used by both triggers. No new i18n keys (reuses `globalSearch.trigger`). `eslint` + the 222-test suite green ; typecheck clean on all touched files.

- 2026-07-04: Forms — standards pass across every app form (correctness fixes + `FieldRow` adoption). Two workstreams. **(1) Correctness**: replaced native `window.confirm()` delete prompts with the shared `ConfirmDialog` in [webhook-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/webhook-editor.tsx>) and [role-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/rbac/role-editor.tsx>) ; in the calendar [new-event-popover.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/new-event-popover.tsx>), replaced the two `window.confirm()` discard prompts + the desktop two-step inline delete with `ConfirmDialog` and unified the desktop footer onto `FormActionsFooter` (was hand-rolled ; mobile already used it) — plus new `calendar.dayView.newEvent.discardTitle`/`discardCta` i18n keys (en + fr) ; unified the [calendar-manage-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-manage-dialog.tsx>) desktop footer onto `FormActionsFooter` ; added the missing loading `Skeleton` to [admin-notifications.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/[id]/admin-notifications.tsx>) ; swapped the [trusted-devices-section.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/trusted-devices-section.tsx>) hand-rolled confirm `Dialog` for `ConfirmDialog` and localized its two busy strings. **(2) `FieldRow` adoption**: migrated the hand-rolled `<Label>+control+help` blocks to the shared [`FieldRow`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/field-row.tsx) (wrapped in `@container` for the responsive label-left switch) across the organization editor, webhook editor, RBAC role editor, admin + self-service profile forms, and the calendar manage dialog — keeping each form's existing `useState`/`DirtyFormBar`/autosave model and custom widgets (logo/avatar upload, `SubscriptionPicker`, permission grid, `ColorInput`, role list) untouched. The data forms (projects/industries) adopt `FieldRow` in parallel. Auth flows (email/password/TOTP), the notification toggle matrices, and the typed-email danger-zone gate were assessed and intentionally left as-is (poor fit for a flat field layout). Verified : `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green on all touched files ; the calendar-manage dialog visually confirmed via the screenshot harness (mobile + desktop).

- 2026-07-04: User detail panel — fixed the banner's viewport bleed leaking into the detail `Sheet`. The `UserBanner` hero stretches full-viewport-width via `w-screen` + `ml-[calc(50%-50vw)]` (right for the full-page `/account/profile` + `/admin/users/[id]` surfaces), but inside the resizable detail panel it forced the banner to 100vw — producing a horizontal scrollbar with dead space on the right and shoving the avatar off the panel's left edge. Added a `bleed` prop to [`UserBanner`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/user-banner.tsx) (default `true` ; the inner rows fill the container instead of re-constraining to a page gutter when off), threaded through [`AdminProfileForm`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/[id]/admin-profile-form.tsx>) and set to `!inPanel` in [`user-detail.tsx`](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/[id]/user-detail.tsx>). In the panel the banner now stays within the panel width (no horizontal scroll) and the avatar sits in the content gutter ; the full-page surfaces keep the edge-to-edge bleed. No i18n / behavior changes ; `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green, 222 web tests pass.

- 2026-07-04: Global search — app-wide ⌘K command palette. Added a command palette reachable from anywhere via ⌘K / Ctrl+K or a search-field-shaped trigger at the top of the primary-nav drawer ([global-search/](monark/app/services/web/src/components/global-search/)). `GlobalSearchProvider` (mounted in the [(authed) layout](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/layout.tsx>)) owns the open state + the single global keydown handler and renders the dialog once for the whole authed tree ; `GlobalSearchTrigger` (in [primary-nav-menu.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/primary-nav-menu.tsx)) opens it via context and shows a platform-aware Kbd (⌘ on Apple, Ctrl elsewhere). Results are two cmdk groups (built on the existing [`ui/command`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/command.tsx), extended with `shouldFilter` / `label` pass-through) : (1) **contextual** — the current section's entities live from the api (`projects.list` search on `/data/projects`, client-filtered `industries.list` on `/data/industries`, `calendar.events.search` on `/calendar`), each navigating to its detail deep-link ; (2) **navigation** — a "Go to" list of app sections (admin routes gated on `rbac.isAdmin`) filtered by the query. cmdk's built-in filtering is off since results are already filtered server-side / here. The calendar's own local ⌘K binding was removed so it no longer double-fires with the global one ([calendar-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-shell.tsx>)) ; its toolbar Search button + `CalendarSearch` popover stay as a page-scoped affordance. New `globalSearch.*` i18n (trigger, placeholder, group + route labels) in en + fr. `tsc --noEmit`, `eslint`, and the 222-test suite green.

- 2026-07-04: Filter menu — searchable option lists. A field's value submenu in [`FilterMenu`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) now shows a search box at the top once its option list is long enough (> 8 options, or forced via a new per-filter `searchable?` flag), on both the desktop dropdown (sticky search, filtered radio / checkbox list) and the mobile modal section. Matching is case-insensitive against each option's label (or an explicit `searchText` when the label is a non-string node), with a "no results" empty state. Keeps the long taxonomy pickers (e.g. the projects Industries filter) usable without scrolling. New optional `searchPlaceholder` / `noResults` on `FilterMenuLabels` (text-free component ; both data callers pass the shared `filters.*` strings), new `filters.searchPlaceholder` + `filters.noResults` i18n keys in en + fr. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-04: Detail panels — resizable width + adaptive field-label layout. Two ergonomics wins for the data-model detail panels. (1) The desktop `Sheet` in [`TableDetailLayout`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-detail-layout.tsx) is now **resizable** : its left edge is a pointer drag handle (grip on hover, like the DataTable column handle) that sets an explicit width overriding `panelClassName`'s `sm:max-w-*` cap ; the chosen width persists to `localStorage` as one shared preference across every list screen (clamped to `[380px, 95vw]`). Mobile stays full-screen (no handle). (2) New shared [`FieldRow`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/field-row.tsx) moves a form field's label **above** the control when tight and to the **left** (a 10rem label column) once there's room — driven by a **container query** (`@container` on the form + `@lg:` on the row), so it responds to the panel's own resized width rather than the viewport, and every field in the form flips together (no per-field mix, consistent alignment). Ported the two data forms ([project-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/projects/project-form.tsx>), the industry form in [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/industries/industries-list.tsx>)) onto it. No i18n / behavior changes ; `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green, 222 web tests pass.

- 2026-07-04: Tooling — i18n key-parity CI gate. New [tools/check-i18n.ts](monark/app/tools/check-i18n.ts) (`pnpm check:i18n`, wired into the CI repo-checks job and the pre-PR gate) flattens every locale catalog under [services/web/src/messages](monark/app/services/web/src/messages) to its dotted key set and fails if any locale drifts from `en` : a key present in one locale but missing in another (which renders a silent runtime fallback), or a dead key present only in a translation. Value equality is intentionally **not** checked — many en/fr strings are legitimately identical (brand names, ICU skeletons, cognates), so comparing values would be noise. Locks in the current 1279-key en/fr parity and brings any future locale (e.g. `es.json`) under the same gate automatically. Second of the "make conventions executable" changes from [docs/review/collaboration.md](monark/app/docs/review/collaboration.md).

- 2026-07-04: DataTable — config menu reworked into Columns + Sorting submenus. The sliders (⚙) button previously opened a flat `DropdownMenu` of visibility checkboxes, and reordering was only possible by dragging the column _headers_ (undiscoverable). It now opens a **Popover** ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)) with a root list that drills into two sub-panels : **Columns** — one row per data column with a **drag handle on the left** (reorder) and a **visibility checkbox** ; and **Sorting** — **multi-level**: an ordered priority list of active sort fields, each with a drag handle to reprioritize, an Ascending / Descending toggle, and a remove (×) button, plus an "Add sort field" section listing the remaining sortable columns. It's wired to the table's `sorting` state (already an ordered array, so multi-column sort works natively) so header-click sort and the menu stay in sync. A Popover hosts it rather than a menu because dnd-kit's pointer-drag fights a `DropdownMenu`'s item / roving-focus semantics ; the Columns panel and the Sorting priority list each use a vertical `SortableContext`, and column order + visibility + the full sort priority persist to localStorage. Header-drag still works. The Sorting submenu is opt-in via a new optional `labels.sort` object (all-or-nothing so there are no English fallbacks in the component) ; added `table.{sorting,sortAscending,sortDescending,sortNone,sortAddField,sortRemove}` i18n (en + fr) and passed `sort` from all 9 `DataTable` consumers. The drag handle's accessible name reuses the column's already-translated header. Added `table-config-{root,columns,sorting}` + `table-sorting-active` stories to the [screenshot harness](monark/app/services/web/tools/screenshots/stories.tsx) ; web `tsc` + `eslint` green, 222 tests pass.

- 2026-07-04: Filters — submenu-per-field + data-type-aware inputs in [`FilterMenu`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx). The dropdown previously listed every field _and_ all its values in one long flat list ; now each field is a top-level row that opens its own **submenu** (with a muted summary of the current value on the row), so the menu stays short. `FilterConfig` became a union discriminated by `type` so the value input matches the field's data type : `select` (default when `type` is omitted → radio list ; existing consumers unchanged), `multiSelect` (checkboxes, `value: string[]`), `text` (text input) and `date` (date input). Desktop renders the submenu ; mobile keeps the full-screen modal but with a labelled, type-aware section per field. Back-compatible : all four current consumers (projects, industries, admin users, admin webhooks) pass plain select configs and needed no change. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green ; 222 web tests pass.

- 2026-07-04: Data section — grouped the custom data models under one nav home. Projects and Industries are no longer separate top-level primary-nav entries ; a single **Data** entry now leads to a `/data` section with its own secondary nav (the model list), mirroring the `/admin` shell. Routes moved from `/projects` + `/industries` to `/data/projects` + `/data/industries` (the app is pre-release, so old links simply 404). New shared shell at [(authed)/data/](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/data/>): `layout.tsx` (AppBar + secondary nav + full-width content, since data models render wide tables rather than the max-w-2xl admin forms), `page.tsx` (redirects to the first model), and the `DATA_TABS` config + `DataSidebar` / `DataTabsBar` (reusing the shared [Sidebar](monark/app/services/web/src/components/sidebar.tsx), exactly like AdminSidebar/AdminTabsBar). The moved per-model layouts are now permission-gate-only (their `<model>.read` check stays; the AppBar/main come from the Data layout). The secondary nav lists only the models the user can read: `DATA_TABS` carries each model's `permission`, and the Data layout resolves `myPermissions` server-side and passes the allowed model ids into `DataSidebar` / `DataTabsBar`, so an inaccessible tab never renders (no client-side flash) while the route gate stays the real boundary. Primary nav swapped to a `Data` entry (Database icon) in [config/primary-nav.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/config/primary-nav.ts) ; all internal links (`useDetailPanelRoute` base paths, primary-column open-panel + full-page hrefs, form close redirects, `[id]` back-links, the `new` redirect) retargeted to `/data/*` ; new `data.tabs` + `appBar.primaryNav.items.data` + `appBar.breadcrumb.{data,projects,industries}` i18n keys in both locales. `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` green.

- 2026-07-02: Build fix — client error boundary pulling in `server-only`. `(authed)/error.tsx` is a Client Component (all Next error boundaries are), but it rendered the **server** `BrandedAppLogo` wrapper, which transitively imports [lib/trpc-server.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/trpc-server.ts)'s `import "server-only"` — failing the build with "You're importing a component that needs server-only". Switched it to the client-safe [`BrandedAppLogoView`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/branded-app-logo-view.tsx) with static template-brand data (an error screen can't fetch the singleton-org logo server-side anyway). Confirmed it was the only client component importing the server wrapper ; `pnpm --filter web build` is green again.

- 2026-07-02: Tooling — module-completeness CI gate. New [tools/check-modules.ts](monark/app/tools/check-modules.ts) (`pnpm check:modules`, wired into the CI repo-checks job and the pre-PR gate) asserts every module is structurally complete : any package exporting a `*Router` is registered in [modules.manifest.ts](monark/app/modules.manifest.ts) (the exact drift that let the calendar router ship unmanifested), and every manifest module has a README, a `contracts/events.ts` if it has a router, and a `tests/integration/` suite. Conscious, temporary exceptions live in an `ACKNOWLEDGED_GAPS` allowlist that the gate **fails on once the gap is closed**, so the debt stays visible in code and self-cleans (current entries : calendar's missing event contract, and the calendar + projects integration suites). Also wrote the missing [packages/projects/README.md](monark/app/packages/projects/README.md). First of the "make conventions executable" changes from [docs/review/collaboration.md](monark/app/docs/review/collaboration.md).

- 2026-07-02: Security + release-readiness — fixed the review-flagged blockers and cleared the pre-PR gate. **Auth** : the feature-flags tRPC router ([feature-flags/src/server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/feature-flags/src/server/index.ts)) exposed override mutations as `publicProcedure` with an input-supplied `actorId` ; they now gate on `feature-flags.write` / `read` and derive the actor from the session, closing an unauthenticated security-control bypass. **Tenancy** : a cross-tenant IDOR in the calendar router ([calendar/src/server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/index.ts)) let any authenticated user edit / delete any event by id ; `events.update` / `events.delete` now verify the event's org + calendar accessibility (mirroring `events.create`) via a new `findCalendarEventById` ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/data.ts)). **Codegen** : registered `@monark/calendar` in [modules.manifest.ts](monark/app/modules.manifest.ts) (the generated router + event union were drifted and failed `gen --check`) and decoupled core `@monark/notifications` from the calendar notification kind ([router.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/server/router.ts)) so the package typechecks standalone. **UX** : added app error boundaries ([(authed)/error.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/error.tsx>), [global-error.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/global-error.tsx)) and a `DataTable` error-state-with-retry ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)) wired into all seven list screens, so a failed query no longer masquerades as an empty list ; fixed the webhook create flow to reveal the one-time signing secret before navigating ([webhook-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/webhook-editor.tsx>)). Also cleared pre-existing lint debt (dead `eslint-disable` directives for rules the project never configured ; unused vars) to green the gate. New `error.*` + `table.loadError` / `table.retry` i18n keys (en + fr). Full review under [docs/review](monark/app/docs/review/README.md).

- 2026-07-01: Calendar — archive parity for calendars (soft-delete → Archive + Restore). The calendar UI called soft-delete "Delete" with copy claiming events were "permanently removed … cannot be undone", and had no restore path. Added the missing capability : [data.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/data.ts) gained `restoreCalendar` + an `includeDeleted` option on `listAccessibleCalendars`/`findCalendarById` ; the router ([index.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/index.ts)) gained a `calendars.restore` procedure (gated on `calendar.delete`/`calendar.manage`) and an `includeDeleted` input on `calendars.list` (managers only). The Prisma `Calendar.deletedAt` column already existed, so no schema change. UI is self-contained in [calendar-sidebar.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-sidebar.tsx>) : delete relabelled to **Archive** (non-destructive confirm, honest copy), plus a manager-only "Archived" section (its own `includeDeleted` query) with a **Restore** action — the three view files and `CalendarDef` are untouched. Manage-dialog delete relabelled to "Archive calendar". New `calendar.sidebar.{archiveCalendar,archiveConfirm*,archivedSection,noArchived,restoreCalendar,restore*}` i18n in both locales ; old `deleteCalendar`/`deleteConfirm*` keys now dead. Events keep soft-delete as-is (no natural list surface to expose restore ; deferred). Note : calendar is not in [modules.manifest.ts](monark/app/modules.manifest.ts) (in-progress module), so its entry in the generated `app-router.generated.ts` is hand-maintained ; `pnpm gen:routers` strips it until it's added to the manifest.

- 2026-07-01: Perf — trimmed redundant per-navigation server work. Two behaviour-preserving wins on the request hot path. (1) The bootstrap-status read was fetched twice on every render — once in the root [layout.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/layout.tsx) (org brand color) and again in the gate layouts via `isSystemBootstrapped()` ; wrapped it in a React `cache()` reader (`getBootstrapStatus` in [bootstrap-gate.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/bootstrap-gate.ts)) so both call sites share one api round trip per request. (2) The client [TrpcProvider](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/trpc-provider.tsx) QueryClient had no default `staleTime`, so every mounted query refetched the instant it remounted during a soft navigation (the App Router keeps the QueryClient alive across routes) ; set a 30s default `staleTime` — mutations still `invalidateQueries` so data stays correct, this only drops the redundant refetches when bouncing between screens. (3) The [authed layout](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/layout.tsx>) ran its post-auth reads serially — `isCurrentDeviceTrusted` (itself two serial api calls) then `users.me` ; now the device gate and the deletion-grace `users.me` read fire together via `Promise.all` (the device check still redirects before `me` is used, so `me` is only wasted in the rare revoked-device case), and inside [trusted-device-cookie.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/trusted-device-cookie.ts) the feature-flag check and device lookup are dispatched in the same tick on one client so `httpBatchLink` folds them into a single round trip. Net: the authed layout's post-auth work drops from ~4 serial round trips to ~2 parallel ones, with identical redirect behaviour. The duplicate `getUser` (middleware + layout) was left as-is on purpose — Supabase's SSR guidance requires the layout to re-validate via `getUser` rather than trust the cookie-read session for authorisation.

- 2026-07-01: Dev — enabled Turbopack for `next dev`. Switched [services/web/package.json](monark/app/services/web/package.json)'s `dev` script to `next dev --turbopack`. On webpack, on-demand dev compilation of a cold route in this workspace took ~33s (measured on `/signin`), so navigating to any not-yet-compiled page stalled 15-30s+ ; Turbopack compiles the same route in ~0.7s (warm requests ~0.2s) — a ~45x cold-compile speedup with no code changes. Safe because [next.config.ts](monark/app/services/web/next.config.ts) has no custom `webpack()` hook (only `transpilePackages` / `serverExternalPackages` / `headers` / the `next-intl` plugin, all Turbopack-native). Production builds are unchanged (still webpack on Vercel). Restart `pnpm dev` to pick it up.

- 2026-07-01: Color — one standard color input across the app. Every "pick a color" affordance was bespoke : Calendars had a swatch that opened the full `ColorPicker` in a popover, while Roles and Organizations had a bare hex `<Input>` + a static preview square and **no picker at all**. New shared [`ColorInput`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/color-input.tsx) composes the existing [`ColorSwatch`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/color-swatch.tsx) + [`ColorPicker`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/color-picker.tsx) into a controlled swatch-opens-picker control with an editable hex field beside it (`swatchOnly` to drop the field ; a dashed swatch for the empty/unset state ; `defaultColor` seeds the picker when unset). Adopted it in [calendar-manage-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-manage-dialog.tsx>), [role-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/rbac/role-editor.tsx>), and [organization-detail.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/organizations/[id]/organization-detail.tsx>) — so Roles and Organizations gain the picker, and all three read the same. Added shared `common.save` + `common.chooseColor` i18n keys (en + fr ; the picker's Save/Cancel now go through i18n instead of the English defaults) and a `color-input` story to the [screenshot harness](monark/app/services/web/tools/screenshots/stories.tsx). No schema / RBAC / behavior change.

- 2026-07-01: Projects — align destructive action with industries on the "archive" model. Projects and industries back onto the same server module (both have soft-delete + `restore` + hard-delete), but the projects UI exposed only a bare "Delete" (a soft-delete mislabeled, with copy promising a restore that had no UI), while industries used Archive / Restore / Delete-permanently. Brought [projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/projects/projects-list.tsx>) to parity : **Archive** (soft, `projects.delete {id}`) and **Delete permanently** (`{hard:true}`) as row actions gated on `deletedAt`, a **Restore** action (`projects.restore`) on archived rows, an "Archived" `FilterMenu` toggle wired to `includeDeleted`, and an "Archived" badge on archived rows (non-clickable). Dropped the delete danger-zone from [project-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/projects/project-form.tsx>) so archiving lives on the list exactly like industries. New `admin.projects.{archive,hardDelete,restore,archivedBadge,archivedFilterLabel,activeOnly,includeArchived,emptyArchived}` i18n + updated `actions` in both locales (mirroring the industries wording) ; the old `form.delete*` keys are now dead. No server/router change (capability already existed). Note : roles + webhooks are genuine hard-deletes ("Delete role/endpoint" is accurate) and users has a distinct grace-deletion lifecycle, so those keep "Delete" ; calendar has the inverse mismatch (soft-delete behavior but "permanently removed" wording with no restore UI) and is left as a separate follow-up.

- 2026-07-01: Dropped the permanent scrollbar-gutter reservation. Removed `html { scrollbar-gutter: stable }` from [globals.css](monark/app/services/web/src/app/globals.css) — it reserved horizontal gutter space for the vertical scrollbar on every page (even short ones that never scroll) and diverged from stock shadcn, which leaves overlay scroll-locking to Radix's `react-remove-scroll` (it compensates for the scrollbar width itself when a modal opens). The unrelated `body { overflow-x: clip }` bleed-element safety net stays. Trade-off : centered content can shift a few pixels between a tall (scrollbar) and short (no scrollbar) route, the behavior the reservation had masked ; we accept it to match shadcn and avoid the gutter's side effects.

- 2026-07-01: Admin + list screens — unified list/detail UX ("best of both"). Converged the two screen generations onto one house style so the older admin surfaces and the newer projects/industries screens read and behave the same. **Save** : `DirtyFormBar` (the dirty-gated sticky "unsaved changes" banner) is now the single save affordance ; gained a `containment="viewport" | "container"` prop ([dirty-form-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dirty-form-bar.tsx)) so it anchors to a detail panel (`TableDetailLayout`'s `SheetContent` is now `relative`) as well as full-page, and replaced the always-visible `FormActionsFooter` in [project-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/projects/project-form.tsx>) + [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/industries/industries-list.tsx>) `IndustryFormContent` + the hand-rolled buttons in [industry-edit-form.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/industries/[id]/industry-edit-form.tsx>) (delete moved to a `DangerCard`). **Filters** : the admin toolbars dropped their bespoke code for the shared `FilterBar` + `FilterMenu` — [users-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/users-list.tsx>) shed ~180 lines of `DropdownMenuSub` + hand-built filter chips, [webhooks-manager.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/webhooks-manager.tsx>) and [organizations-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/organizations/organizations-list.tsx>) followed. **Tables** : `DataTable` gained a calm chrome default (idle sort glyph + column-layout menu stay hidden until hover) and a `leading` primary-column slot for avatars/logos ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx), [types.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/types.ts)) ; every admin `<ul>` card list migrated to it (organizations, rbac roles, webhooks, webhook deliveries, and the heterogeneous users list — invites + users merged into one discriminated-union row with revoke as a row action). **Navigation** : the four admin sections now open records in an in-place `TableDetailLayout` panel like projects/industries, keeping the full `[id]` page as an "open full page" escape hatch — the existing editors ([role-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/rbac/role-editor.tsx>), [webhook-editor.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/webhook-editor.tsx>), [organization-detail.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/organizations/[id]/organization-detail.tsx>), [user-detail.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/users/[id]/user-detail.tsx>)) gained a `containment` prop that drops their `PageHeader`, anchors the save bar to the panel, and closes the panel on success instead of routing. Added `columns.*` + panel (`collapsePanel` / `openFullPage` / `panelTitle`) i18n keys in both locales ; the old `admin.*.filters.{openAria,menuLabel,clearChipAria}` keys are now dead. No RBAC / schema / event changes.

- 2026-07-01: Typecheck — cleared the web baseline to green. `pnpm --filter web exec tsc` had a backlog of pre-existing errors ; fixed them so a new web type error is now a real signal. Declared `react` as a peer + dev dependency on [@monark/calendar](monark/app/packages/calendar/package.json) (pnpm's strict node_modules had never linked react into the package, so its two react-importing files couldn't resolve the module) ; replaced `Array.findLastIndex` (ES2023, outside the lib target) with a typed helper in [week-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/week-view.tsx>) ; widened the `listCalendarMembers` return type to include the `avatarUrl` it already selects ([data.ts](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/server/data.ts)) ; added the `reminders` include to `searchCalendarEvents` to match its `CalendarEventRow[]` return ; dropped the `DaySchedule` type from the client barrel (it collided with the component export ; still exported from `@monark/calendar/contracts`) ; coerced `eventType` when rebuilding a focused `CalendarEvent` ; dropped the mismatched `initialData: initialCalendars` (trimmed to `CalendarDef` in page.tsx) across day/week/month views in favor of the existing `?? initialCalendars` fallback ; and guarded two `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` spots in [color-picker.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/color-picker.tsx). The full turbo `typecheck` still fails on one **unrelated** in-progress issue : `@monark/notifications` `router.ts` hardcodes a `case "calendar.event.reminder"` that isn't in `NotificationKind` when the core package compiles standalone (belongs to the calendar-reminders notification feature ; needs module-provided sample data). Added calendar stories (Edit Calendar modal, sidebar chip strip, form footer) to the [screenshot harness](monark/app/services/web/tools/screenshots/stories.tsx) for visual validation.

- 2026-07-01: Forms — consistent footer action order + full-screen modal close affordance. Standardized the shared [`FormActionsFooter`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/form-actions-footer.tsx) so every create/edit form reads the same way : the destructive **Delete** is pinned far-left and **Cancel + Save** are right-aligned with Save rightmost (`[Delete] … [Cancel] [Save]`), replacing the previous save-left / delete-right order (affects all admin forms + the calendar mobile modals). Full-screen (`mobileFullScreen`) modals now always render the built-in top-right **X** in the title bar (dropped the `hideClose` on the calendar [Edit Calendar](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-manage-dialog.tsx>) and [Create/Edit Event](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/new-event-popover.tsx>) modals ; headers get `pr-12` so the title clears the X). No i18n / schema / behavior changes beyond layout.

- 2026-07-01: Mobile — stop wide content from blowing out the layout. The page-level safety net (`body { overflow-x: clip }`) already prevented a runaway horizontal scrollbar, but wide children (a `DataTable`, or a responsive grid missing a base `grid-cols-1`) were getting clipped instead of shrinking, because flex / grid children default to `min-width: auto`. Hardened the shared [`DataTable`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) root with `w-full min-w-0` so it always fits its parent and scrolls internally in any flex / grid context, and fixed the two responsive grids that lacked a shrinkable base column ([dev/fields gallery](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/dev/fields/page.tsx>), [delivery-detail.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/admin/webhooks/[id]/deliveries/[deliveryId]/delivery-detail.tsx>)) by adding `grid-cols-1` + `min-w-0`. Convention going forward : a `*:grid-cols-N` always pairs with a base `grid-cols-1`, and a flex / grid item holding wide content carries `min-w-0`. No behavior / i18n changes.

- 2026-07-01: Admin list screens — mobile-first detail panel + filter toolbar. The table ↔ detail-panel pattern and the toolbar above it were desktop-only in feel ; on a phone the right-hand `Sheet` was a cramped sliver and the loose `Select` filters overflowed the row. `TableDetailLayout` now renders the panel **full-screen and modal** below `md` (overlay + `useIsMobile`-gated sizing) instead of a persistent non-modal side sheet ([table-detail-layout.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/table-detail-layout.tsx)) ; selecting a row takes over the screen, so `DataTable`'s primary cell drops inline-edit on mobile and lets the label open the panel (the open-panel icon also stays visible without hover) ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx)), and `PanelHeaderBar` hides its open-full-page link there since the panel is already full-screen ([panel-header-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/panel-header-bar.tsx)). The toolbar filters collapsed behind a single icon button 8px from the search field : `FilterBar` gained explicit `search` / `filter` / `actions` slots ([filter-bar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-bar.tsx), search fills the row on mobile and holds ~350px on desktop), and a new declarative [`FilterMenu`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/filter-menu.tsx) takes `FilterConfig[]` and renders a dropdown of radio groups on desktop / a full-screen modal (title + top-right close) on mobile, with an active-filter count badge on the trigger. Reused the shared `dialog.tsx` `mobileFullScreen` prop (added a `hideClose` option) for the modal. Ported [projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/projects/projects-list.tsx>) (status + industry filters) and [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/industries/industries-list.tsx>) (archived filter, replacing the show/hide toggle) onto the slots ; new shared `filters.*` i18n namespace + industries `archivedFilterLabel` / `activeOnly` / `includeArchived` keys in both locales. No RBAC / schema / event changes.

- 2026-07-01: Fields — reusable, descriptor-driven form inputs + table cells. New [`@/components/fields`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields) toolkit built for the coming polymorphic Data Model : a single field-type registry ([registry.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/registry.tsx) `FieldInput` + [schema.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/schema.ts) + [cells.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/cells.tsx)) drives both a schema-driven [`AutoForm`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/auto-form.tsx) and matching data-table columns via [`fieldColumn`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-column.ts). Covers all field kinds (text, long text, rich text / WYSIWYG, number, boolean, date, datetime, single-select, multi-select with optional custom values, relation, url, email) with an RHF input per type ([inputs/\*](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs)) wrapped in a shared [`FieldShell`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/field-shell.tsx) that formalizes the copy-pasted label + control + help + error block ; validation flows from zod fragments assembled by `schemaForFields`. Single/multi-select support opt-in colored `badges` and relation supports `avatars` (image or initials). The `richText` field is a headless [Tiptap](https://tiptap.dev) editor ([rich-text-field.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/inputs/rich-text-field.tsx)) storing sanitized HTML, with a [`RichTextView`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/fields/rich-text-view.tsx) DOMPurify-sanitized read-only renderer and scoped `.rich-text-content` styles in [globals.css](monark/app/services/web/src/app/globals.css). Adopts react-hook-form for the app (first use) and ports the shadcn `command` / `form` / `radio-group` primitives into [components/ui](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui) ; added `react-hook-form`, `@hookform/resolvers`, `cmdk`, `@radix-ui/react-radio-group`, `@tiptap/*`, `dompurify` to [services/web/package.json](monark/app/services/web/package.json). New `fields.*` i18n chrome + validation keys in both locales ; text-free components take already-translated field labels as props. Dev gallery at [(authed)/dev/fields](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/dev/fields/page.tsx>) exercises every type in a form and a table ; unit coverage in [tests/lib/fields.test.ts](monark/app/services/web/tests/lib/fields.test.ts). No RBAC / schema / event changes ; the descriptor is designed to map 1:1 onto a server field definition when the polymorphic layer lands.

- 2026-07-01: Calendar — responsive, touch-first mobile experience. The calendar had zero responsive handling ; on a phone the 275px sidebar and the multi-column grids overflowed and every create/move/resize gesture was mouse-only. Below Tailwind's `md` (768px), the shell now collapses to **Day view only** — Week/Month render as Day via a new `effectiveView` while the URL's `?view=` is preserved so a wide viewport restores the choice, and the view switcher is hidden ([calendar-shell.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-shell.tsx>)). The fixed sidebar becomes a horizontal calendar-toggle strip above the grid (mini date-picker dropped ; toolbar arrows navigate) via a `hidden md:flex` aside plus an `md:hidden` chip strip in [calendar-sidebar.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-sidebar.tsx>), with [day-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/day-view.tsx>)'s shell stacking `flex-col md:flex-row`. Tapping a chip toggles its calendar's visibility ; a **long-press** (450ms, cancelled by a scroll drag) opens a bottom `Sheet` drawer exposing the desktop `…` menu's edit / delete actions (reusing the existing delete `AlertDialog` and `editCalendar` / `deleteCalendar` i18n keys). Day columns are now `min-w-[85vw] md:min-w-[300px]` ([day-column.tsx](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/client/ui/day-column.tsx), [day-columns-area.tsx](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/client/ui/day-columns-area.tsx)) so one calendar fills the phone and extras scroll horizontally. Drag-to-move / drag-to-resize migrated from mouse-only to **Pointer Events** across the shared [day-event-block.tsx](monark/app/packages/calendar/src/client/ui/day-event-block.tsx) and both [day-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/day-view.tsx>) / [week-view.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/week-view.tsx>) document listeners, so gestures work under touch as well as mouse ; draggable surfaces carry `touch-none` and the resize handle is revealed on select / coarse-pointer devices. Tap-to-create is unchanged. On mobile the calendar forms also go full-screen for better use of the viewport and no longer autofocus a field on open (which would raise the keyboard before the form is visible) : a new opt-in `mobileFullScreen` prop on the shared [dialog.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/dialog.tsx) drives the Edit Calendar dialog ([calendar-manage-dialog.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/calendar-manage-dialog.tsx>)), and the Create/Edit Event form ([new-event-popover.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/new-event-popover.tsx>)) renders as an anchored `Popover` on desktop but a full-screen `Dialog` with a single-column layout on mobile (`onOpenAutoFocus` prevented, `useIsMobile`-gated so the wrapper is chosen once without a flash). The full-screen modals are a flex column with a left-aligned title header, a scrollable top-left content area, and a pinned bottom action banner built from the shared admin [`FormActionsFooter`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/form-actions-footer.tsx) (event delete pairs it with [`ConfirmDialog`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/confirm-dialog.tsx)), so the calendar forms read like the rest of the app's create/edit surfaces. New SSR-safe [`useIsMobile`](monark/app/services/web/src/hooks/use-is-mobile.ts) hook drives the behavioral view-switch and the modal wrappers. No new i18n strings, no RBAC / schema / data changes.

- 2026-07-01: Inline cell editing + primary-column open-panel affordance in `DataTable`. Non-computed cells can now be renamed in place: a column `edit` config (`getValue` / `onSave`, optional `maxLength` / `placeholder`) makes the cell click-to-edit — a text input commits on Enter / blur (only when the trimmed value changed) and reverts on Escape ([data-table.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) `EditableCell`). Because the primary column's label is now editable, opening the detail panel moved to a `PanelRight` icon button that reveals on cell hover (was: the label itself being a link). Wired projects (rename title) and industries (rename name + slug) to their partial-update tRPC mutations ([projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/projects/projects-list.tsx>), [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/industries/industries-list.tsx>)) ; new `table.openPanel` i18n key in both locales. Enum / relational / timestamp columns stay read-only.

- 2026-06-30: Reusable admin table + list/detail patterns. Extracted the projects / industries admin screens onto a shared pattern set under [services/web/src/components/patterns](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/): `FilterBar` + `FilterBarSearch`, `PanelHeaderBar` (h-14, aligned to the appbar), `FormActionsFooter`, `ConfirmDialog`, and the `TableDetailLayout` + `useDetailPanelRoute` table ↔ non-modal-`Sheet` combo (persistent panel; closes on Escape / outside-click but not when clicking a row, a `…`/column menu, or a spawned confirm dialog). New standard [`DataTable`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/patterns/data-table/data-table.tsx) built on `@tanstack/react-table` + `@dnd-kit` with sortable / hideable / resizable / drag-reorderable columns persisted per-browser in localStorage ; a mandatory pinned **primary column** (main label + optional subtext, label optionally a link that opens the detail panel) that can't be hidden or moved ; and per-row actions collapsed into a single trailing `…` menu. Refactored [projects-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/projects/projects-list.tsx>) and [industries-list.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/industries/industries-list.tsx>) onto it (industries lost its two near-duplicate archive / hard-delete dialog components ; projects gained a row-level delete). Added `@tanstack/react-table` + `@dnd-kit/{core,sortable,utilities}` to [services/web/package.json](monark/app/services/web/package.json) ; new `table.*` + per-screen `actions.*` i18n keys in both locales. Selected row keeps the primary-tinted ring highlight from the earlier selection work.

- 2026-06-14: Calendar module — Phase 2: Calendar data model, per-calendar RBAC, tRPC router, sidebar with mini monthly calendar and calendar list, and event edit mode. Prisma models `Calendar`, `CalendarRoleAccess`, `CalendarEvent` added; `calendar.view` / `calendar.manage` permissions registered; full CRUD tRPC router wired at `calendar.*`; `CalendarSidebar` (Create CTA, `react-day-picker` mini calendar, calendar list with manage dialog) added left of the timeline; clicking any `DayEventBlock` now opens the popover in edit mode with ring-primary outline; contracts updated to `startAt`/`endAt` `Date` fields and new `CalendarDef` type.

- 2026-06-14: Calendar module — Day View. First module with a primary nav surface. Renders a vertical 24h timeline with configurable columns (title, description, avatar + day schedule), a real-time current-time indicator (primary-color dot + line), and click-to-create event flow snapped to 15-minute increments with a popover form (title, time range, location, participants, description). Pure client-state in this phase; persistence layer deferred. Package scaffold at [packages/calendar](monark/app/packages/calendar/); wired UI at [services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/calendar/>).

- 2026-05-29: User-configurable trusted-device window + one-click revoke from email. Two security-UX upgrades to the trusted-device flow that land together because they share the new-device-email surface.
  - **Configurable trust window.** New `User.trustedDeviceTtlDays` column (default 90 days) lets each user pick 30 / 60 / 90 days from `/account/security`. The RFC 6265bis 400-day cookie ceiling is still enforced at write-time via `clampDeviceTtlDays` but isn't offered in the UI — 90 days is plenty for most active users, longer than that drifts past the point where "did I trust this device on purpose?" stops being a meaningful question. Migration [20260529180000_add_trusted_device_ttl_days](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260529180000_add_trusted_device_ttl_days/migration.sql) ; backend reads it in [`recognizeOrRegister`](monark/app/packages/auth/src/server/trusted-devices.ts) and returns the effective TTL alongside the cookie value so [`recognizeDeviceAfterAuth`](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/trusted-device-cookie.ts) sets the cookie's `Max-Age` to match the row's `expiresAt`. New tRPC mutation `users.updateTrustedDeviceTtl` retroactively slides every active device's `expiresAt` forward by the new TTL from `lastSeenAt` so "tighten my window" takes effect immediately. UI Select wired into [TrustedDevicesSection](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/account/trusted-devices-section.tsx>) above the device list, with i18n strings in both locales. The 400-day ceiling mirrors RFC 6265bis' cookie `Max-Age` cap — anything higher would be silently truncated by the browser.
  - **One-click revoke link in new-device emails.** The "If that wasn't you, revoke this device" CTA in `auth.new-device` no longer dumps the user on `/account/security` to hunt for the right row — it now lands on `/auth/revoke-device/<token>` which revokes the specific device with one click. Token is an HMAC over `{ purpose, userId, deviceId, expiresUnixSec }` signed by the new `EMAIL_ACTION_SECRET` env var (32-char dev default with a `logger.warn` ; production must override). Helper lives at [packages/auth/src/server/email-action-token.ts](monark/app/packages/auth/src/server/email-action-token.ts) with constant-time signature compare. New publicProcedure [`auth.trustedDevices.revokeByEmailToken`](monark/app/packages/auth/src/server/index.ts) verifies the token + revokes ; the action is idempotent so a second click on the same link is a no-op. Web page at [services/web/src/app/auth/revoke-device/[token]/](monark/app/services/web/src/app/auth/revoke-device/[token]/) deliberately renders a confirm button instead of firing on page load — link-prefetchers (email-scanner bots, browser "preload on hover") would otherwise revoke before the human clicks. Token data shape declared in the notification registry for `auth.new-device` so the subscriber-injected `revokeLink` overrides `globalVars()`'s generic `/account/security` link only for this kind.
  - **Backlog items filed in [docs/todo/backlog.md § Trusted devices](monark/app/docs/todo/backlog.md#trusted-devices)** : step-up auth on sensitive actions during the first 24h after a new-device sign-in (mitigates password-only users), out-of-band approval flow (banks pattern, high-friction), and per-IP rate-limit on the revoke endpoint.

- 2026-05-29: DB-side expiry on `TrustedDevice`. Before this, the browser cookie's 400-day `Max-Age` was the only signal that walked a device out of trust — the row itself lived forever and any cookie that somehow outlived its `Max-Age` (manual restore, browser bug, attacker-copied cookie) would still grant trust on lookup. New `expiresAt TIMESTAMP NOT NULL DEFAULT (now() + INTERVAL '400 days')` column on [packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) + index for the planned sweep cron. Migration [20260529170000_add_trusted_device_expiry](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260529170000_add_trusted_device_expiry/migration.sql) backfills existing rows from `lastSeenAt + INTERVAL '400 days'` (devices last seen 2+ years ago land already-expired, ready for the sweep). Code-side : new `DEVICE_TRUST_TTL_MS` constant in [packages/auth/src/server/trusted-devices.ts](monark/app/packages/auth/src/server/trusted-devices.ts) mirrors the cookie's `Max-Age` ; `recognizeOrRegister` sets the row's `expiresAt` on create + slides it forward on every recognized sign-in (matching the cookie's sliding refresh) ; every "is this device still trusted" query (`recognizeOrRegister` lookup, `listTrustedDevices`, `findCurrentDeviceId`, `shouldChallengeTotp`, `markDeviceTotpVerified`) now filters by `expiresAt: { gt: new Date() }`. Sweep cron + `/account/security` UI surface filed in [backlog](monark/app/docs/todo/backlog.md#trusted-devices) — schema gives us the cut now, cron + UI close the loop in a follow-up.

- 2026-05-29: Integration test isolation hardening. Diagnosed orphan org rows (`ff-org-a`, `ff-org-b`, `test-org-webhooks`) sitting in the dev DB — left over from [packages/feature-flags/tests/integration/is-enabled.test.ts](monark/app/packages/feature-flags/tests/integration/is-enabled.test.ts) and [packages/webhooks/tests/integration/data.test.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/tests/integration/data.test.ts), both of which seed orgs in `beforeAll` with no matching `afterAll` cleanup. Anything that bypassed `vitest.integration.config.ts`'s `globalSetup` (IDE extension picking the unit config, ad-hoc `vitest run`) hit whatever `DATABASE_URL` was in the env — usually the dev Supabase. Fix landed in two layers : (a) new [packages/test-utils/src/assert-test-db.ts](monark/app/packages/test-utils/src/assert-test-db.ts) setupFile aborts the run with a clear error if `DATABASE_URL` doesn't contain the testcontainer marker `/monark_test` (the path component baked in by `startTestDb`'s `.withDatabase("monark_test")`) ; wired into all 7 `vitest.integration.config.ts` files via `setupFiles: ["@monark/test-utils/assert-test-db"]` and exported from `@monark/test-utils/package.json`. (b) `afterAll` cleanup in both leaking suites so the testcontainer exits clean even when the assertion is bypassed. Re-ran both suites after the fix : 30 + 35 tests still green, dev DB still has the single legit `monark` org row.

- 2026-05-08: Production-readiness sweep + dev DX papercuts. Closes the four production hard-blockers from the prior assessment + the three local-dev pain points surfaced over the last few sessions.
  - **Render Blueprint for the api + scheduled jobs** ([render.yaml](monark/app/render.yaml)). One Web Service for the api (`tsx src/server.ts`, port 4000, `/health` healthcheck). Two Cron Job services hit the api with the shared `CRON_SECRET` : `monark-cron-deletions` runs `/cron/process-account-deletions` daily at 03:00 UTC ; `monark-cron-webhook-sweep` runs `/cron/sweep-webhook-deliveries` every 5 min as the external backstop for the in-process worker. Closes the GDPR-class "deletion cron unscheduled" gap. Migrations run via `preDeployCommand` (Prisma `migrate deploy`) so a failing migration aborts the deploy without corrupting the running version. Secrets are marked `sync: false` ; Render prompts the operator on first deploy. New `start:prod` script in [services/api/package.json](monark/app/services/api/package.json) drops the `--env-file=.env` flag so Render's native env injection takes over.
  - **Vercel deploy spec for web** ([services/web/vercel.json](monark/app/services/web/vercel.json) + [services/web/DEPLOY.md](monark/app/services/web/DEPLOY.md)). Build + install commands run from the workspace root (`pnpm gen && pnpm --filter web build`) so workspace packages resolve. `ignoreCommand` skips the build when nothing under `services/web/` or its `@monark/*` dependencies changed (api-only PRs don't burn a Vercel deploy). DEPLOY.md documents the manual UI steps (project root, region pin, `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` env vars).
  - **Production security headers** ([services/web/next.config.ts](monark/app/services/web/next.config.ts) + [services/web/src/middleware.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/middleware.ts)). Added a `headers()` config that returns HSTS (2 years, includeSubDomains, preload-eligible), CSP, X-Frame-Options=DENY, X-Content-Type-Options=nosniff, Referrer-Policy=strict-origin-when-cross-origin, Permissions-Policy (camera/microphone/geolocation/payment all `()`), plus the UA-CH `Accept-CH` / `Critical-CH` opt-in. `connect-src` is built from `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` at build time. Headers return `[]` in dev — HSTS would lock subsequent http://localhost requests and the strict CSP would block Next's HMR overlay. Moved the previously-middleware UA-CH headers into the same `headers()` block so middleware stays focused on auth state ; the comment block in [middleware.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/middleware.ts) cross-links to the new home.
  - **Webhook secret env-var resolver** ([packages/webhooks/src/server/secret-store.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/src/server/secret-store.ts)). Single-tenant deploys can now ship without an external secret manager. New `setWebhookSecretResolver(fn)` API + a built-in `makeEnvVarSecretResolver()` that reads `WEBHOOK_SECRETS_JSON` (`{ endpointId: plaintext }` map) with a per-endpoint `WEBHOOK_SECRET_<endpointId>` env-var fallback. Endpoint ids are validated against `^[a-z0-9]+$` defensively before being interpolated into env-var names so a hostile id can't reach into unrelated env vars. `resolveSecret` now consults the registered resolver only after the in-memory cache + activeStore miss, so the dev file-backed flow + just-rotated secrets (cached for the current process) keep working. Wired into [services/api/src/server.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/server.ts)'s `startBackgroundWork` BEFORE `startWebhookDeliveryWorker()` so the first delivery already has a path to plaintext. 14 new unit tests in [packages/webhooks/tests/secret-resolver.test.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/tests/secret-resolver.test.ts) cover the JSON map, per-endpoint env vars, malformed-JSON tolerance, endpoint-id validation, and the resolver-throws path.
  - **Trusted-device cookie SameSite=strict in production** ([services/web/src/lib/trusted-device-cookie.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/trusted-device-cookie.ts)). The cookie is the device-recognition signal the (authed) layout reads on every page navigation — flipping to `strict` blocks CSRF-style cross-site rides for it without breaking the dev LAN-cross-origin flow (which keeps `lax`). Gated on `NODE_ENV === "production"`.
  - **`pnpm bootstrap`** ([tools/bootstrap.ts](monark/app/tools/bootstrap.ts)). One command for a fresh checkout : sanity-checks Node 22+ / pnpm / Docker, copies `.env.example` → `.env` for the four services that ship one (never overwrites), runs `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`, boots Supabase (`pnpm exec supabase start`), and applies Prisma migrations. Idempotent — re-running is a no-op or near-no-op at every step. `--no-supabase` flag skips the docker / migrate steps for the rare case where you bring your own Postgres.
  - **Pre-commit hook** (root [package.json](monark/app/package.json) + simple-git-hooks + lint-staged). `simple-git-hooks` registers the hook on `pnpm install` via the `prepare` script ; `lint-staged` runs ESLint with `--fix --max-warnings=0` on staged `.ts` / `.tsx` files and Prettier across staged JS / JSON / YAML / Markdown. Catches the kinds of issues that have been making CI flaky over the last few rounds before they reach the runner. Typecheck stays in CI ; running `tsc --noEmit` on the whole project on every commit was felt as too slow in the original DX scan.
  - **Prisma postinstall graceful on Windows file lock** ([packages/db/scripts/postinstall.mjs](monark/app/packages/db/scripts/postinstall.mjs)). The `pnpm dev` server holds `query_engine-windows.dll.node` open ; running `pnpm install` while dev is up was failing with `EPERM` on the atomic rename and aborting the entire workspace install. New wrapper runs `prisma generate`, detects EPERM/EBUSY/EACCES on Windows specifically, and downgrades to a warning (the existing generated client is still usable). Real generation errors + non-Windows failures keep aborting the install. Linux CI sees no behaviour change.
  - **Net.** Single-tenant production-readiness check : (1) deploy target — done (Vercel + Render). (2) deletion cron — done. (3) webhook secret resolver — done. (4) HSTS / CSP / SameSite=strict — done. The four hard blockers are closed. Multi-tenant stays parked until needed. Local dev now boots with one command and won't fail install just because the dev server is running.

- 2026-05-08: CI wall-time pass. Auto-CI runs were sitting at ~28 min per push ; trimmed to an estimated ~3:30–4 min by separating concerns and parallelising the verify gate.
  - **Playwright e2e moved to manual-trigger only** ([.github/workflows/e2e.yml](monark/app/.github/workflows/e2e.yml), [ci.yml](monark/app/.github/workflows/ci.yml)). The full e2e suite (browser install + `supabase start` + the suite itself) costs ~20 min on every push for what's primarily an auth / admin regression net ; the integration suites in the verify gate already cover every data-layer + tRPC contract surface. New workflow file under `workflow_dispatch` only — runs from Actions → E2E → Run workflow when an auth / admin PR needs the regression check. Browser install also dropped from `chromium firefox webkit` → `chromium` (the playwright config only runs Chromium ; pulling the other two with `--with-deps` was costing ~5 min for nothing). Saves ~21 min wall time off the auto-CI path.
  - **Verify gate split into 4 parallel jobs** ([ci.yml](monark/app/.github/workflows/ci.yml)). Was a single sequential job running lint → typecheck → unit → integration → merge → codecov. Now : `lint-typecheck`, `repo-checks` (codegen drift + module-tier rules), `unit-tests`, and `integration-tests` run in parallel ; a final `coverage-upload` job downloads the unit + integration artifacts, fuses them via the merge script, and uploads to Codecov. Wall time = `max(lint-typecheck, unit-tests, integration-tests, repo-checks)` + the upload job, instead of their sum. The integration leg dominates at ~3 min ; everything else clears in under 2 min, so the verify gate now closes in ~3:30 vs the old ~6 min. Coverage artifacts upload at the end of each test job and the merge job downloads both into the per-package `coverage/{unit, integration}/` shape that `tools/merge-coverage.ts` already expects, so no merge-script changes needed. Saves ~2:30 wall time off the verify path.
  - **Net.** Auto-CI on push / PR : ~28 min → ~3:30–4 min. The e2e workflow stays one click away in the Actions tab when needed, and devs run `pnpm test:e2e` locally before touching auth or admin flows. Integration coverage continues to run on every push so the threshold floors stay enforced.

- 2026-05-08: e2e suite hardening. After the seed-step fix landed earlier today, the next CI run surfaced two more failure classes in the e2e job. Pragmatic fixes for both, plus diagnostic logging so the next bounce-loop reveals which gate is firing.
  - **Mailpit ECONNREFUSED** ([services/web/tests/e2e/signup-happy-path.spec.ts](monark/app/services/web/tests/e2e/signup-happy-path.spec.ts)). The signup-happy-path spec was the only one still pointing at `helpers/mailpit.ts` ; CI runs `supabase start` which boots **Inbucket** on 54324, not Mailpit on 8025, so every clear/poll call hit `connect ECONNREFUSED ::1:8025`. Converted the spec to use `helpers/inbucket` (matching the rest of the email-using e2e suite : forgot-password, signup-confirm). Adjusted the mailbox-clear call to take `email` (Inbucket's per-mailbox API vs Mailpit's global flush) and the body fields from `message.HTML` / `message.Text` to the Inbucket-helper-shaped `message.html` / `message.text`. The Mailpit helper file stays for local dev stacks that swap it in, just unused in CI.
  - **Sign-in bounce loop** ([services/web/playwright.config.ts](monark/app/services/web/playwright.config.ts) + [services/web/src/app/(authed)/layout.tsx](<monark/app/services/web/src/app/(authed)/layout.tsx>)). Nine specs failed on the same pattern : signed in successfully → landed on `/account` → immediately bounced to `/signin`. Best hypothesis after reading the gate code without trace access : `pnpm dev` runs `api` and `web` in parallel via turbo, and the previous Playwright config waited only on `http://localhost:3000` to come up. The api can still be booting when web responds, so the seeded sign-in's tRPC calls (trusted-device `recognize`, totp `isChallengeRequired`) intermittently fail and the (authed) layout's `isCurrentDeviceTrusted` then bounces the request through `/auth/sign-out-stale` → `/signin`. Two changes : (1) Playwright's `webServer` now blocks on both `http://localhost:3000` AND `http://localhost:4000/health` before tests launch — the second entry uses `reuseExistingServer: true` so it waits on the api's healthcheck without trying to start a second `pnpm dev`. (2) The (authed) layout now writes a `[(authed)/layout] redirect: <reason>` line to stderr when `MONARK_AUTH_GATE_DEBUG=1`, exposing whether the bounce came from the bootstrap gate, the `getUser`/`getSession` check, or the trusted-device check ; the env var is set on the e2e job. If the api-readiness wait closes the bounce-loop, the debug logging stays out of the test output ; if not, the next failure reveals which path actually fires and we can target the real fix.

- 2026-05-08: e2e seed step fix. The `Seed e2e test users` CI step started failing with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: Cannot find package '@monark/auth'` after a recent dep cleanup. Root cause : `tools/seed-e2e-users.ts` imports `@monark/auth/server` and `@monark/organizations/server` (alongside `@monark/db` and `@monark/rbac`), but the workspace root [`package.json`](monark/app/package.json) only listed `@monark/db` and `@monark/rbac` as direct deps. pnpm's isolated layout only surfaces direct deps at the workspace root's `node_modules/`, so `tsx` couldn't resolve the missing two — locally the script may have worked because of stale linking from earlier installs, but `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` on CI gave it a clean tree where the resolution failed. Added both missing packages as `workspace:*` direct deps. The seed step now resolves cleanly on the next CI run.

- 2026-05-08: Codecov upload fix. The first CI run after the merge-coverage infra landed showed Codecov reporting numbers nobody recognised ; the upload log surfaced two compounding bugs.
  - **`files: |` block was passed as one giant filename.** [`.github/workflows/ci.yml`](monark/app/.github/workflows/ci.yml) used a YAML literal-block scalar to list each per-package lcov path on its own line. codecov-action v4 takes the YAML value verbatim and forwards it as a single CLI arg, so the action received `-f "./packages/auth/coverage/lcov.info\n./packages/branding/..."` (literal `\n` characters embedded). The `not_found_files` warning in the action log shows the entire block as a single missing path. Switched to comma-separated single-line syntax which the v4 uploader splits correctly.
  - **Auto-discovery uploaded 58 conflicting files.** With the explicit `files:` list silently doing nothing, codecov-action v4 fell back to walking the workspace for every `lcov.info` and `coverage-final.json` it could find. With our split coverage layout (`coverage/unit/`, `coverage/integration/`, plus the merged `coverage/`) that meant **three lcov files per package** describing the same source paths with different totals, plus the matching `coverage-final.json` triplets. Codecov's per-source merge logic ended up reporting whichever file won the deduplication race rather than the merged number we intended. Two fixes : (1) `disable_search: true` on the action so only the explicit `files:` list gets uploaded ; (2) `tools/merge-coverage.ts` now `rm -rf coverage/{unit,integration}/` after producing the merged output, so even if `disable_search` is ever flipped back the upload stays clean. Also added the previously-missing `packages/webhooks` and `services/web` paths to the upload list.
  - **Net effect.** Codecov uploads exactly the merged `lcov.info` per package starting on the next CI run ; the dashboard number will reflect the actual measured baseline (matching what `pnpm coverage:merge` prints locally) instead of whichever per-run lcov happened to land last in Codecov's merge.

- 2026-05-08: Webhooks subscribers integration suite. Closes the second-largest 0 % file in the webhooks package — `subscribers.ts` (the wildcard handler that fans every domain event into the outbox table) was uncovered before this. Floor bumped from 50 → 55 lines, 55 → 60 functions in [tools/merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts) ; webhooks merged coverage moved 55 % → 59 % overall, with `subscribers.ts` itself at 93.5 %.
  - **`@monark/webhooks` subscribers** ([packages/webhooks/tests/integration/subscribers.test.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/tests/integration/subscribers.test.ts)). 11 cases against the testcontainer covering every routing rule the wildcard handler enforces. Each spec emits a synthetic event through the real `@monark/common` event bus (`emit(...)`) and counts the resulting `WebhookDelivery` rows ; no mocks — the `findMatchingEndpoints` / `findUserMemberOrgIds` / `findOnlySingletonOrgId` calls all resolve against Postgres so the routing answer reflects what production would compute. Buckets : (1) Registration idempotency — calling `registerWebhookSubscribers()` twice doesn't double-bind the wildcard handler (otherwise every event would fan out 2× and double-deliver). (2) Recursion guard — `webhook.*` events are skipped so a `webhook.delivery-failed` alert doesn't loop back into more deliveries. (3) No-match short-circuit — events with no subscribed endpoints don't enqueue. (4) Rule 1 (platform-tier firehose) — endpoint with `organizationId === null` receives every matching event regardless of payload shape ; covered for both no-payload-id events (e.g. `system.heartbeat`) and org-scoped events. (5) Rule 2 (direct org match) — endpoint at ORG_A receives an event with `organizationId: ORG_A`, does NOT receive one with `organizationId: ORG_B` (the privacy guarantee — over-fan-out across orgs would leak operator-confidential payloads). (6) Rule 3 (user-tied membership match) — user-tied events with no `organizationId` route to org-scoped endpoints whose org the user belongs to (active membership only — ex-members with `leftAt` set are excluded). (7) Singleton-org fallback — when the user has no membership rows but exactly one org exists in the deploy, the singleton's org-scoped endpoint receives the event. The singleton block soft-deletes every other org for its scope and restores them in `afterEach` because the shared testcontainer hosts orgs from sibling test files (data + worker) that would otherwise break the "exactly one" precondition.
  - **Net effect.** `subscribers.ts` lifts from 0 % → 93.5 % covered. Combined with the worker batch earlier today, the webhook delivery seam (subscriber → outbox → worker → receiver) now has end-to-end CI gates from event emission through HTTP attempt + state machine. `router.ts` is the last sizable 0 % file in the webhooks package ; it's tRPC-procedure plumbing that's harder to integration-test without booting a full server, so it'll likely stay covered indirectly via the e2e admin flows rather than via this package's integration suite.

- 2026-05-08: Webhooks worker integration suite. Picks up the load-bearing delivery state machine (`worker.ts` was 0 % covered before this) and locks in every branch the production loop relies on. Floor bumped from 40 → 50 lines in [tools/merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts) ; webhooks merged coverage moved 49 % → 55 % overall, with `worker.ts` itself at 91 %.
  - **`@monark/webhooks` worker** ([packages/webhooks/tests/integration/worker.test.ts](monark/app/packages/webhooks/tests/integration/worker.test.ts)). 10 cases against the testcontainer covering the full delivery state machine. `deliverOne` accepts an injectable `fetchImpl` so we drive every HTTP outcome (2xx success, 4xx retry, 5xx retry, network throw, secret-not-resolved) without standing up a fake receiver. `tickOnce` doesn't accept a fetch override (worker-loop callers don't have one to pass) so the one `tickOnce` case stubs `globalThis.fetch` directly + restores it in a `finally`. Buckets : (1) `tickOnce` returns processed=0 on empty queue, processes a real pending delivery against a 200 receiver. (2) `deliverOne` success path — records attempt + marks delivered + emits `webhook.delivery-succeeded`, resets `consecutiveFailures` to 0. (3) `deliverOne` retry path — records attempt with statusCode + marks pending with `nextAttemptAt` in the future + emits `webhook.delivery-failed{permanent:false}` for 4xx, 5xx, network-throw, and "no plaintext secret available" cases (the secret-store-empty branch records a meaningful error message instead of crashing). (4) Exponential backoff curve — `nextAttemptAt` ≈ `BASE_MS × 2^(attempt-1)` for the first two attempts, never exceeds `WEBHOOK_DELIVERY_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`. (5) Exhausted retries — once `attemptNumber >= WEBHOOK_DELIVERY_FAILURE_LIMIT`, delivery moves to `failed`, emits `webhook.delivery-failed{permanent:true}`, auto-disables the endpoint with reason `"auto-disabled after N consecutive failures"`, and emits `webhook.endpoint-disabled-after-failures`. (6) Already-disabled endpoints don't get re-disabled — the `status !== "active"` guard in `maybeAutoDisable` ensures a manually-disabled endpoint's `disabledReason` doesn't get overwritten.
  - **Net effect.** `src/server/worker.ts` lifts from 0 % → 91 % covered ; `src/server/secret-store.ts` lifts to 51 % (the `rememberSecret`/`resolveSecret` paths get exercised through the worker fixture) ; `src/server/secrets.ts` lifts to 66 % (signing-header generation runs on every successful test). The `subscribers.ts` (event-bus wiring) and `router.ts` (tRPC router) are still at 0 % ; subscribers is the most reachable next target — the rbac / notifications / feature-flags integration suites already established the `_resetSubscribersForTesting` + module-mock pattern that fits.

- 2026-05-08: Users `read` + `metadata` integration suites. Picks up the second-lowest-coverage package on the merged baseline (34 % lines → 81 % lines) by closing the read-wrapper + per-user metadata gaps that the existing `data.test.ts` didn't reach. Floor bumped from 25 → 75 in [tools/merge-coverage.ts](monark/app/tools/merge-coverage.ts).
  - **`@monark/users` read wrappers** ([packages/users/tests/integration/read.test.ts](monark/app/packages/users/tests/integration/read.test.ts)). 9 cases. The `read.ts` exports are thin wrappers over `data.ts` finders, but two of them carry branches worth pinning : `getByIdOrThrow` raises `NotFoundError` on a missing id (the canonical error every router-level handler unwraps to a 404), and `getCurrent` short-circuits to `null` when `ctx.userId` is itself null (the unauthenticated-request branch most server actions guard on first thing). Plus `getById` / `getByEmail` round-trips so the data-layer pass-through is locked in.
  - **`@monark/users` metadata** ([packages/users/tests/integration/metadata.test.ts](monark/app/packages/users/tests/integration/metadata.test.ts)). 20 cases against the testcontainer covering the per-user metadata API end-to-end.
