New `auth.all-devices-revoked` notification kind in [packages/notifications/src/contracts/registry.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/contracts/registry.ts) (SECURITY category, `requiredEmail: true`) with bilingual templates at [packages/notifications/src/templates/auth/all-devices-revoked.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/templates/auth/all-devices-revoked.ts) ; HTML + plaintext + in-app variants. Subscriber wired in [packages/notifications/src/server/subscribers/index.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/server/subscribers/index.ts) calls `notify("auth.all-devices-revoked", ...)` with the revoked count + occurredAt. Dev-overlay test-send synthesises a payload of 3 devices.
- 2026-04-29: Global app bar across authenticated surfaces. New [`AppBar`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/app-bar.tsx) (server component) — sticky top-0, blurred `bg-background/70` background, subtle border-bottom, `max-w-6xl` content width. Left side carries the Monark logo + wordmark linked to `/account` ; right side carries the existing `NotificationsBell` plus a new [`UserMenu`](monark/app/services/web/src/components/user-menu.tsx) (avatar dropdown). The user menu shows the avatar (or initials over the brand orange→red gradient when no avatar is set, matching profile-section's empty-state pattern) and on click opens a dropdown with the user's display name + email as a non-interactive header, then `Account` (link to `/account`) and `Sign out` (invokes the existing `signOutAction` server action via `useTransition`). The previous per-page headers are gone from `/account`, `/account/notifications`, `/account/delete`, `/admin` — each page now mounts `<AppBar />` once and renders its own `<h1>` inside the content area instead of mixing nav with title. Pre-auth surfaces (signin, signup, /auth/confirm-error, etc.) keep their own centered "logo + form" layout ; the appbar implies "you're inside the app." **i18n** (en + fr): new `appBar.{home, userMenu.{triggerAria, account, signOut, signingOut}}` namespace.
- 2026-04-28: Profile skills selector. New `User.skills String[]` column ([migration 20260428100000_add_user_skills](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260428100000_add_user_skills/migration.sql), default `ARRAY[]::TEXT[]`). Canonical set lives in [packages/users/src/contracts/skills.ts](monark/app/packages/users/src/contracts/skills.ts) ; Phase 1 ships with `accounting`, `coding`, `design`, `digital-marketing`, `product` (alphabetical, English ; localised labels in en + fr). `users.updateProfile` zod schema gains a `skills?: Skill[]` field that dedupes + sorts on the way in so persisted order is stable regardless of click sequence ; allowed values are validated against the canonical set so a stale or misspelled key is rejected at the boundary. `UserProfileUpdatedEvent.changed` extended with `"skills"`. **UI** ([profile-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/profile-section.tsx)): toggle-button row between Bio and Locale ; selected skill = `default` variant (filled brand orange), unselected = `outline`. Each click fires `updateProfile.mutate({ skills })` immediately and a local `skills` state mirrors the server so the button doesn't flicker between click and confirmation. Stale rows from a deprecated skill are filtered out at hydration so the toggle UI stays valid even if `SKILLS` shrinks later. **i18n** (en + fr): new `account.profile.labels.skills` + `account.profile.skillLabels.{accounting,coding,design,digital-marketing,product}` namespace. fr labels: Comptabilité / Programmation / Design / Marketing numérique / Produit. **Adding a new skill** is one PR: append to `SKILLS` + add the matching label to both message catalogs ; no migration.
- 2026-04-28: Banner UX matched to avatar + interactive image cropping. **Banner mirrors the avatar pattern** ([profile-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/profile-section.tsx)) ; the banner surface itself is the click target wrapped in a `DropdownMenuTrigger`. Hovering darkens it (35% black) and reveals an `Upload` icon (empty state) or `Pencil` icon (with-banner state) ; clicking opens a dropdown with the same Replace / Remove items the avatar dropdown carries. The previous static top-right buttons are gone. **Avatar empty-state icon** ; the hover overlay now shows `Upload` when no avatar is set and `Pencil` only when one already exists. **Format / size hint paragraphs removed** ; "JPEG, PNG ou WebP. Jusqu'à 5 Mo ; recadrée au format 3:1" + the avatar equivalent are gone in favour of the on-image hover affordance + crop dialog. Upload errors still surface inline below the banner so the user knows why a pick was rejected. **Interactive crop dialog**: new [components/image-crop-dialog.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/image-crop-dialog.tsx) (built on `react-easy-crop`) opens whenever the user picks a source image. Drag-to-position + scroll-to-zoom + a `<Slider>` for finer zoom control, circular crop shape for the avatar (1:1) and rectangular for the banner (3:1) so the dialog visually matches the destination shape. On confirm, the cropped region is rendered to a `<canvas>` (rotation-aware), exported as a `Blob`, wrapped as a `File` (preferred MIME = source's, JPEG fallback for un-encodable formats like HEIC), and handed to the existing upload action. Output width is capped at 1500px so a 6000px-wide phone photo doesn't ship a giant blob to the server (sharp still resizes, this just avoids wasted network). The server-side sharp pipeline is untouched ; it now receives an already-cropped image so its center-crop becomes a no-op while WebP conversion + final dimensions stay enforced. **Components installed from `@monark/ui`**: `dialog.tsx`, `slider.tsx` mirrored into [services/web/src/components/ui/](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/) ; deps `@radix-ui/react-dialog`, `@radix-ui/react-slider`, `react-easy-crop` added to web. **i18n** (en + fr): new top-level `imageCrop.{cancel,confirm,processing,zoomAria}` namespace for the dialog ; new `account.profile.{avatarCropTitle,avatarCropHint,bannerCropTitle,bannerCropHint,bannerUploadAria,bannerEditAria}` ; removed `avatarHint`, `bannerHint`, `bannerEmpty` keys.
- 2026-04-28: Hover-feedback audit across interactive surfaces. The /account vertical tabs had no hover state because the base `TabsTrigger` only carried an active-state rule ; the cursor-pointer pass surfaced this when buttons stopped looking dead but tabs still did. **Base `TabsTrigger`** (registry + [services/web/src/components/ui/tabs.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/tabs.tsx)) now carries `hover:bg-muted/60 hover:text-foreground`. The 60% opacity is deliberate so consumers that override the active state to the same `bg-muted` (the AccountShell's vertical tabs, by spec) still keep a clear delta between "hovered" and "active" ; the active rule wins on the active row via data-attribute specificity, so nothing else has to change. **Inbox filter buttons** ([inbox.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/notifications/inbox.tsx)) now use the same `hover:bg-muted/60 hover:text-foreground` pattern on inactive states, with `transition-colors` for smoothness. **NotificationsSection toggle** ([notifications-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/notifications-section.tsx)) gained per-state hover (`bg-primary` → `hover:bg-primary/85`, `bg-muted` → `hover:bg-muted/70`), conditionally attached so the disabled / forced state has zero hover delta — disabled toggles shouldn't pretend to be interactive. **Audit confirmed already-good**: `Button` variants (`hover:bg-primary/90` etc.), `AccordionTrigger` (`hover:underline`), `Toggle`, `SidebarMenuButton`, `dropdown-menu` / `context-menu` / `menubar` / `select` / `command` items (Radix maps hover to `data-[highlighted]` / focus, and the items already have `focus:bg-accent`), `NotificationsBell` button + popover rows, `CollapsibleSection` header, profile avatar pencil overlay (`group-hover:bg-black/45`), inline link-style buttons (sign-out, TOTP cancel, mark-read, dismiss). **Audit deliberately skipped**: `Switch` (state-color flip + thumb position is the feedback ; an extra hover bg is noisy), `Checkbox` / `RadioGroup` (small targets ; the click-target's `<label>` is the hover surface in practice), `<a>` anchors (browser handles).
- 2026-04-28: Cursor audit across the registry + the app. Tailwind v4 dropped `cursor: pointer` on `<button>` and shadcn followed the spec on menu-style items (`cursor: default` to feel native), but the result reads broken on web — clickable surfaces don't tell you they're clickable. Reverted that across the board. **Registry** ([monark/ui/components/ui/](monark/ui/components/ui/)): `button.tsx`, `tabs.tsx` (TabsTrigger), `accordion.tsx` (AccordionTrigger), `toggle.tsx`, `checkbox.tsx`, `radio-group.tsx`, `select.tsx` (Trigger + Item), `command.tsx` (Item), `dropdown-menu.tsx` (Item / CheckboxItem / RadioItem / SubTrigger), `context-menu.tsx` (same surface), `menubar.tsx` (Trigger + SubTrigger + Item / CheckboxItem / RadioItem), `sidebar.tsx` (sidebarMenuButtonVariants), `connect-wallet.tsx` (custom trigger) all gain `cursor-pointer`. Items previously carrying `cursor-default` (the menu/select families) flipped to `cursor-pointer`. Form controls already had `disabled:cursor-not-allowed` ; left untouched. `switch.tsx` already had the right shape (`cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed`) — used as the model for the rest. Resize cursors on `sidebar.tsx`'s rail kept as-is (correct semantic). `select.tsx` scroll-up/down buttons kept on `cursor-default` (auto-scroll on hover, not a click). **App-installed copies** ([services/web/src/components/ui/](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/)): `button.tsx`, `tabs.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx` mirrored. **App custom components**: bell button, mark-all-read inline button + disabled-state, popover list rows ([notifications-bell.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/notifications-bell.tsx)) ; filter All/Unread toggle, mark-read + dismiss inline buttons ([inbox.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/notifications/inbox.tsx)) ; help-icon trigger next to category labels ([notifications-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/notifications-section.tsx)) ; avatar edit trigger + disabled state ([profile-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/profile-section.tsx)) ; sign-out and TOTP-cancel form buttons ([account/page.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/page.tsx), [signin/totp/page.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/signin/totp/page.tsx)) ; dev-overlay collapsible-section header ([collapsible-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/collapsible-section.tsx)). **What stayed**: anchors (`<a>`), since browsers already render pointer ; text inputs / textareas (text cursor is correct) ; native `<label>` over inputs (browser handles).
- 2026-04-27: Profile + notifications polish. **Profile bio**: new `User.bio String?` column ([migration 20260427180000_add_user_bio](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260427180000_add_user_bio/migration.sql)), plumbed through `users.updateProfile` (zod max 400 chars, blanks → null, code-point counted via `Array.from`), `UserProfileUpdatedEvent.changed` extended with `"bio"`. UI ([profile-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/profile-section.tsx)) gets a `<Textarea>` with a live `n/400` counter that turns red past the limit ; blur-saves like the display-name field. **Avatar edit moved onto the avatar itself** ; the previous "Avatar" form row is gone. Hovering the avatar darkens it (45% black) and reveals a pencil icon ; clicking opens a `DropdownMenu` (newly installed from monark/ui registry) with Replace / Remove for an existing avatar, or just Upload for the empty state. Errors still surface inline below the banner. **Banner buttons**: with a banner, Replace + Remove become icon-only secondary buttons (rotate-ccw + trash2, 32px square, aria-labelled). Without a banner, the upload button keeps its label and gains a leading `ImagePlus` icon. **Trusted device title localized** ; [packages/auth/src/server/index.ts](monark/app/packages/auth/src/server/index.ts) now parses `userAgent` once via `UAParser` and ships `browserName` + `osName` as separate fields on `TrustedDeviceView`. The `/account` consumer formats them with `t("deviceTitle", { browser, os })` (en: "{browser} on {os}", fr: "{browser} sur {os}"), falling back to `t("deviceUnknown")` ("Unknown device" / "Appareil inconnu"). The English-only `device.label` baked at insert time is no longer rendered. **Notification timestamps relative**: new [src/lib/format-time.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/lib/format-time.ts) wraps `Intl.RelativeTimeFormat` with locale-aware bucket selection (seconds → minutes → hours → days → months → years), used in the bell popover, the full inbox page (with absolute-time tooltip via `title=`), and the dev-overlay panel. **Dev test-send button labels localized**: dev-overlay notifications panel now uses `t("kinds.<kind>")` instead of the raw kind id (kept on `title` for the dev who wants to see the technical key). **Notification category descriptions**: each category label in [notifications-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/notifications-section.tsx) gets a circled-question-mark `Popover` (newly installed from monark/ui registry) with a multi-sentence explanation of what the category covers + why it matters (en + fr). **Components installed from `@monark/ui`**: `popover.tsx`, `dropdown-menu.tsx`, `textarea.tsx` mirrored into [services/web/src/components/ui/](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/) ; deps `@radix-ui/react-popover` and `@radix-ui/react-dropdown-menu` added to web. en + fr message catalogs extended throughout (`profile.labels.bio`, `profile.placeholders.bio`, `profile.avatarUploadAria`, `profile.avatarEditAria`, `notifications.kinds.*`, `notifications.prefs.categoryDescriptions.*`, `notifications.prefs.categoryHelpAria`, `trustedDevices.deviceTitle`, `trustedDevices.deviceUnknown`). Migration must be applied with `pnpm --filter @monark/db exec prisma migrate deploy` before the bio field reads cleanly. _On notification email implementation_: yes, the EMAIL channel is end-to-end live ; `notify()` calls `sendMail()` which uses `nodemailer` against `SMTP_URL` (Mailpit at `smtp://localhost:1025` in dev, real SMTP in prod). Without `SMTP_URL` set, the dispatch path log-only-falls-back and the in-app row still persists. The dev overlay's "send test" buttons exercise the full path.
- 2026-04-27: API request logging cleaned up + secured. **`@monark/common` logger** ([packages/common/src/log.ts](monark/app/packages/common/src/log.ts)) now wraps pino with a `redact` allow-list (`req.headers.authorization`, `req.headers.cookie`, `req.headers['set-cookie']`, `req.headers['x-supabase-auth']`, `req.headers['x-api-key']`, `req.headers['x-csrf-token']`, `req.headers['proxy-authorization']`, plus payload-side `*.password`, `*.token`, `*.accessToken`, `*.refreshToken`, `*.access_token`, `*.refresh_token`, `*.tokenHash`, `*.secret`, `*.secretCipher`, `*.cookieValue`, `*.cookieHash`) ; values are replaced with `[REDACTED]` before any line is emitted. In dev (NODE_ENV != production AND stdout is a TTY) the logger now uses `pino-pretty` for coloured one-liners (level-coloured, ms-precision time, hostname/pid/service hidden) ; prod and CI / piped stdout still emit machine-parseable JSON. **API request logger** ([services/api/src/lib/http-logger.ts](monark/app/services/api/src/lib/http-logger.ts), new) replaces the bare `pinoHttp({ logger })` call ; one concise line per request (`POST /trpc/users.me 200 23ms`) at a status-coded log level (info 2xx/3xx, warn 4xx, error 5xx), so pino-pretty's level coloring lights up failures automatically. Bodies + full headers are no longer in the structured payload at all ; only `id`, `method`, `url`, `statusCode`, `responseTime` are kept. **Query strings stripped** before logging — tRPC GET queries encode their input as `?input=…`, which can carry call-time values like `cookieValue` (trusted-device lookup) ; we now log the path only, so the cookie hash never lands in any sink. **`/health` muted** to keep container probes / uptime checks from drowning real traffic. `x-request-id` honoured if the upstream sets it, otherwise a UUID. **Next config**: `pino` and `pino-pretty` added to `serverExternalPackages` in [next.config.ts](monark/app/services/web/next.config.ts) so the worker-thread transport (resolved via `require.resolve`) doesn't get statically bundled by webpack. `pino-http` is no longer imported directly from `services/api/src/server.ts` ; the wrapper module owns all pino-http config.
- 2026-04-27: Notifications follow-ups. **Avatar overlap fix** ([profile-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/profile-section.tsx)) ; the banner box no longer carries `overflow-hidden`, the banner image is clipped via its own absolute-positioned wrapper, and the hint paragraph below gets `pt-12` to clear the avatar's bottom 40px. Avatar is now centered on the banner's bottom edge (Twitter-style: half above, half below) instead of being cropped at the bottom. **Dev-overlay notifications panel** ([panels/notifications.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/dev-overlay/panels/notifications.tsx)) ; lists the unread count + 5 most-recent in-app rows, exposes a "send test" button per registered kind (synthetic data per kind ; `notify()` is called server-side and the result reports delivered/skipped counts), plus mark-all-read and reset-prefs shortcuts. Backed by a new `notifications.dev.testSend` + `notifications.dev.listKinds` tRPC namespace that 404s under `NODE_ENV=production` so the surface stays stable across environments without leaking. **Bell on /admin** ([admin/page.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/admin/page.tsx)) ; the admin home now ships a header with the Monark logo, the unread bell, and an "Account" link, so admins can see notifications without bouncing back to /account. devOverlay i18n catalog gained the `notifications.*` namespace in en + fr.
- 2026-04-27: Notifications core module (Phase 1 step 10) ; first cut. **Package**: new `@monark/notifications` workspace package with `/server`, `/client`, `/contracts` exports and a Phase 1 README. Registered in `modules.manifest.ts` as a core-tier module ; auto-wired into the app router via `pnpm gen:routers` and into `DomainEvent` via `pnpm gen:events`. **Schema**: `Notification` + `NotificationPreference` models + `NotificationChannel` (`IN_APP`, `EMAIL`) and `NotificationCategory` (`SECURITY`, `ACCOUNT`, `ACTIVITY`, `DIGEST`) enums under the new `// ── MODULE: notifications ──` banner in [packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma) ; migration at [migrations/20260427150000_add_notifications](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260427150000_add_notifications/migration.sql). **Dispatch**: `notify(kind, recipient, data)` resolves the user's locale + per-(category, channel) prefs (force-on for SECURITY × EMAIL when `requiredEmail`), de-dupes within a 60s window keyed by `sha256(data)`, renders the kind's TS template module wrapped in the brand `EMAIL_SHELL` partial, persists a `Notification` row per channel, transports via `sendMail` (nodemailer behind `SMTP_URL`, falls back to log-only), and emits `notification.created` / `notification.delivery-failed`. Never throws ; failures land on `failedAt` + `failureReason` so triggering actions can't be rolled back by a notification problem. **Templates**: 7 Phase 1 kinds (`auth.new-device`, `auth.password-changed`, `auth.totp-enabled`, `auth.totp-disabled`, `account.email-changed`, `account.deletion-scheduled`, `account.deletion-canceled`), each with en + fr slots + html + text + inapp surfaces. Shared brand chrome in [\_partials/email-shell.ts](monark/app/packages/notifications/src/templates/_partials/email-shell.ts) reuses the orange header band, Monark wordmark, brand-orange CTA and footer from the Supabase confirmation template ; new emails are "fill in the body slot." **Subscribers**: `registerNotificationSubscribers()` listens to `trusted-device.added`, `user.password-changed`, `totp.enabled`, `totp.disabled`, `user.email-changed`, `user.deletion-requested`, `user.deletion-canceled` and forwards each to `notify()`. **Mailer relocation**: `mailer.ts` + `new-device-email.ts` moved out of `@monark/auth/server` into the notifications package ; `@monark/auth` no longer depends on `nodemailer`. The api boot now calls `registerNotificationSubscribers()` instead of `registerNewDeviceEmailListener()`. **tRPC**: `notifications` router with `unreadCount`, `list` (cursor-paginated), `markRead`, `markAllRead`, `dismiss`, plus `preferences.get` (returns resolved category × channel cells with `forced` flag), `preferences.set`, `preferences.reset`. **UI** ([services/web](monark/app/services/web)): header bell at `/account` (popover with unread badge in brand red `#EF3620`, recent 6, mark-all-read, "Open inbox" link ; polls every 60s + on focus), full inbox at [/account/notifications](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/notifications/page.tsx) (cursor pagination, all/unread filter, per-row dismiss), and a fourth tab on `/account` (`?tab=notifications`) hosting [NotificationsSection](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/notifications-section.tsx) — table of category × channel toggles with SECURITY × EMAIL pre-disabled and a "Reset to defaults" action. Custom-styled toggles instead of pulling in `@radix-ui/react-popover` for one site. en + fr message catalogs extended with the `account.notifications.*` namespace (incl. plural-aware bell aria-label). **Tests** (25 new): template renderer (interpolation, locale branching, unknown-token preservation — 9), prefs resolution (defaults, overrides, force-on, channel-not-declared — 6), registry shape invariants (Phase 1 set present, every SECURITY kind requires email, every kind has a TEMPLATES entry with en+fr inapp subjects — 5), email transport (SMTP detection, log-only fallback, transport caching, error swallow with structured result — 5). **Migration must be applied locally** with `pnpm --filter @monark/db exec prisma migrate deploy` before the runtime reads the new tables. **Limitations**: no real-time subscription channel (60s polling + window-focus refetch only) ; no bell on routes outside `/account` (the app has no global header yet ; lift when one exists) ; DIGEST category has UI but no cron yet.
- 2026-04-27: Profile banner + brand-gradient avatar ring. **Avatar component**: [components/ui/avatar.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/components/ui/avatar.tsx) and the registry source [monark/ui/components/ui/avatar.tsx](monark/ui/components/ui/avatar.tsx) now wrap the Radix root in a span carrying a `padding: 2px` linear-gradient (135°, `#F0870C` → `#EF3620`), so every avatar instance gets the brand orange→red ring by default. Opt out with `<Avatar bordered={false}>` for dense lists where the ring is visual noise. **Profile banner**: new `User.bannerUrl String?` column ([prisma/migrations/20260427100000_add_user_banner_url/migration.sql](monark/app/packages/db/prisma/migrations/20260427100000_add_user_banner_url/migration.sql)), extended `users.updateProfile` schema + `UserProfileUpdatedEvent.changed` enum to carry `"bannerUrl"`. New `uploadBannerAction` / `removeBannerAction` server actions in [account/actions.ts](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/actions.ts) ; uploads accept JPEG/PNG/WebP up to 5 MiB, sharp normalises to 1500×500 WebP at q82 (3:1 cover crop), stored under `avatars/<userId>/banners/<ts>.webp` so the existing avatars-bucket RLS policies (folder[1] = auth uid) continue to gate writes without a second bucket. **Profile UI** ([profile-section.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/profile-section.tsx)): banner sits at the top of the profile card (bleeds to card edges via `-mx-6`, h-32 → h-40 md), avatar floats out the bottom-left Twitter-style, banner controls (Upload / Replace / Remove) overlay top-right. Empty state shows a faint primary→accent gradient at 15% opacity. en + fr message catalogs extended with `banner*` keys. Migration must be applied locally with `pnpm --filter @monark/db exec prisma migrate deploy` before the new field reads cleanly.
- 2026-04-27: Branded transactional emails. Signup confirmation ([supabase/templates/confirmation.html](monark/app/supabase/templates/confirmation.html)) and email-change ([supabase/templates/email-change.html](monark/app/supabase/templates/email-change.html), new) now ship a Monark-branded layout: 560px single-column table-based shell, brand-orange header band (#F0870C), Nunito Sans wordmark, primary CTA button matching the app's button style, the 6-digit code in a code-block surface, plain-text fallback color. Templates branch on `{{ .Data.locale_preference }}` so en + fr render the user's preferred language. Locale plumbing: `signUpUser` now stores `locale_preference` (and `display_name` when present) in Supabase `user_metadata` at signup; [updateLocaleAction](monark/app/services/web/src/app/account/actions.ts) mirrors the value into Supabase whenever the user toggles locale in /account so subsequent emails (email-change, future password-reset) render in the current language. Email-change template wired via new `[auth.email.template.email_change]` block in [config.toml](monark/app/supabase/config.toml); subject "Confirm your Monark email change". Recovery / magic_link / invite / reauthentication templates not yet branded ; tracked for the password-reset and invite passes.
- 2026-04-25: Phase 1 unit-test coverage expansion. Pure helpers extracted to standalone modules so the suite doesn't pull in Prisma/Supabase: `recordVerifyAttempt` + `TotpRateLimitError` to `packages/auth/src/server/totp-rate-limit.ts`, `pickLocaleFromHeader` to `packages/auth/src/contracts/accept-language.ts`, `mostSpecific` exported from `packages/feature-flags/src/server/resolve.ts`, `hashCookieValue` / `mintCookieValue` / `labelFromUserAgent` exported from `packages/auth/src/server/trusted-devices.ts`, `buildAnonymizedEmail` exported from `packages/auth/src/server/account-lifecycle.ts`. New vitest files: `tests/totp-rate-limit.test.ts` (8 cases — first attempt, decrement, cap+1 block, 60s window aging, per-user scoping, Date.now default, history bounded, error shape), `tests/accept-language.test.ts` (10 cases — null/empty/missing, primary tag, region stripping, q-weight ranking, malformed q, case-insensitivity, sloppy headers), `tests/trusted-devices-helpers.test.ts` (11 cases — known SHA-256, determinism, base64url shape, 32-byte length, ua-parser labels for Chrome/Safari/Firefox), `tests/mailer.test.ts` (5 cases — log-only when SMTP_URL unset, transport creation, SMTP_FROM default, transport caching, error swallowing), `tests/signup-schema.test.ts` (9 cases — minimum/full payload, email/password/displayName validation, locale enum, unknown-field stripping), `tests/account-lifecycle.test.ts` (3 cases — uuid format, @monark.invalid domain invariant, no collisions). `packages/feature-flags/tests/resolve.test.ts` (9 cases — precedence ladder user > role > org > global with each layer absent + first-wins on conflict) and `tests/flags.test.ts` (8 cases — auth defaults pinned ON, experimental flags pinned OFF, descriptions non-empty, isKnownFlag prototype-safety). **E2E**: Mailpit polling helper at `services/web/tests/e2e/helpers/mailpit.ts` (search + body fetch + URL extraction) and `signup-happy-path.spec.ts` (gated behind `E2E_FULL_STACK=1`, drives signup form → polls Mailpit for verification subject → follows the link → asserts post-confirm URL). Suite total: 65 auth + 18 feature-flags + 10 rbac = **93 unit tests passing** (was 27 before this pass). Lint + typecheck green across all 11 packages.
- 2026-04-25: Phase 1 polish pass. **Sign-in status banners** ; new `<SignInStatusBanner />` reads `?emailChanged=1` and `?deletionScheduledAt=<iso>` from the URL and surfaces a primary or amber inline banner so the user knows why they're back at /signin. **TrustedDeviceCard `isCurrent` wired** ; new `auth.trustedDevices.currentDeviceId({ cookieValue })` query + `findCurrentDeviceId()` helper resolve the matching device server-side (cookie value never round-trips back to client memory); /account threads the resolved id through `AccountShell` → `TrustedDevicesSection` so the active row gets a "This device" pill. **/admin gates non-admins too** ; new `rbac.isAdmin` query + layout check redirects authenticated non-admins to `/account` before any staff surface renders, in addition to the existing TOTP enforcement gate. **Custom 404** ; branded [`not-found.tsx`](monark/app/services/web/src/app/not-found.tsx) with home + account CTAs and en/fr copy. **Skeleton loading states** ; replaced "Loading…" text in EmailSection (input shape) and TrustedDevicesSection (card-shaped placeholders matching the device cards) using the new `@monark/skeleton` primitive. **Accept-Language sniff at signup** ; `signUpAction` parses the `Accept-Language` header (q-weighted, picks highest supported tag), forwards via the new optional `localePreference` field on `signUpInputSchema` ; `signUpUser` writes it to `User.localePreference` so first paint after verify renders in the user's browser language. **`?totpRequired=1` cleared after first paint** ; `<AdminTotpBanner />` uses `router.replace` to drop the param after mount; refresh keeps the banner only when the live `auth.totp.adminEnforcement` query says it's still required. **turbo.json** ; `test` task drops the bogus `coverage/**` outputs (vitest doesn't run with --coverage), kills "no output files" warnings on every build. CI test step audited and confirmed already wired (was assumed missing).
- 2026-04-25: Phase 1 hardening pass. **Admin TOTP soft-wall** ; new `auth.totp.adminEnforcement` tRPC query + `adminTotpEnforcement(userId)` helper read by [services/web/src/app/admin/layout.tsx](monark/app/services/web/src/app/admin/layout.tsx) (redirects admins missing TOTP to `/account?totpRequired=1&tab=security`) and an amber/destructive `<AdminTotpBanner />` on `/account`. Hard-wall after 7 days computed from the earliest `RoleAssignment.grantedAt`; banner copy escalates accordingly. New `rbac.adminAssignmentSummary(userId)` returns `{ hasAdmin, earliestGrantedAt }`. **Sharp avatar processing** ; uploads now go through `sharp.rotate().resize(512, 512, { fit: "cover" }).webp({ quality: 80 })` before hitting Storage so every avatar is uniform 512×512 WebP regardless of input. **TOTP rate limiting** ; `verifyTotpCode` enforces 5/min per user via in-memory cap, throws `TotpRateLimitError` over budget. **Stale TOTP enrollment cleanup** ; `cleanupStaleTotpEnrollments()` drops un-activated enrollments > 24h old. **Hard-delete pipeline** ; new `account-lifecycle.ts` exposes `hardDeleteUser(userId)` (anonymizes email/displayName/avatar + calls `supabase.auth.admin.deleteUser` + emits `user.deleted` with `previousEmail`) and `processExpiredDeletions()` (iterates the 14d-grace cohort). **Per-device session revocation** ; new `DeviceSession` Prisma model linking trusted devices to Supabase session ids (extracted from JWT `session_id` claim no-verify decode), recorded on every `recognize`, walked on `revoke` to call `supabase.auth.admin.signOut(sessionId)` per session before deleting the link rows. **New-device email** ; `registerNewDeviceEmailListener()` subscribes to `trusted-device.added` and sends a transactional via the new `mailer.ts` (nodemailer behind `SMTP_URL`, defaults to log-only when unset; Mailpit at `smtp://localhost:1025` for dev). **Cleanup** ; dropped diagnostic `console.log`s from `/auth/confirm`. **Tests** ; vitest unit suites for AES-GCM crypto roundtrip + tamper rejection (9 cases), password offline rules / scoring / unicode handling (9 cases), rbac permissions matrix + role ranking (9 cases). **Playwright E2E smoke** scaffolded ([services/web/playwright.config.ts](monark/app/services/web/playwright.config.ts) + [tests/e2e/auth-routing.spec.ts](monark/app/services/web/tests/e2e/auth-routing.spec.ts)) ; covers public auth pages render, /account + /admin redirect when anon, /signin/totp redirect when no challenge pending. Full happy-path (signup → Mailpit verify → TOTP enroll) deferred. IP-to-country geo lookup deferred.
- 2026-04-25: Refactored `/account` into a three-tab layout ("Profile & preferences", "Account & security", "Danger zone") with URL-synced state (`?tab=`), vertical `TabsList` on desktop + horizontal on mobile, lucide tab icons. Installed `@monark/avatar`, `@monark/tabs`, `@monark/sonner`, `@monark/theme` via shadcn CLI. Ported `monark/ui/styles/theme.css` (brand orange `oklch(0.723 0.168 59.48)` + 5-hue chart palette) directly into `globals.css` because the published registry's `theme.json` lags the local source-of-truth. Added heading font-weight + letter-spacing rule from the theme spec. `<Toaster />` wired into the root layout; timed "saved" spans replaced with toasts across profile / password / email-change / totp / danger-zone. Profile avatar now uses `<Avatar />` + `<AvatarFallback />` instead of a hand-rolled div. Trimmed `font-mono` out of product UI; kept on dev overlay surfaces and on opaque tokens (TOTP base32 secret, recovery codes).
- 2026-04-25: Extracted `TrustedDeviceCard` to `services/web/src/components/trusted-device-card.tsx` with a reusable, schema-agnostic prop shape (label, userAgent, first/lastSeenAt, lastSeenIp, country, totpVerifiedAt, isCurrent, onRevoke, size, labels). Uses lucide `Laptop` / `Smartphone` / `Tablet` glyphs auto-detected from the UA string, outlined card with muted glyph chip, TOTP-verified badge, optional "this device" pill, and destructive revoke slot. Both `/account` trusted-devices section and the dev overlay panel now consume this. Intent: lift to `@monark/ui` as `trusted-device-card` once dogfooded.
- 2026-04-25: Added avatar upload to `/account`; `avatars` Supabase Storage bucket declared in `supabase/config.toml` (public-read, 2 MiB cap, JPEG/PNG/WebP only), SQL migration `20260425000000_avatars_bucket_rls.sql` adds `storage.objects` RLS so authenticated users can only insert/update/delete under `avatars/<auth.uid()>/*`, `uploadAvatarAction` / `removeAvatarAction` server actions route through the cookie-authenticated SSR Supabase client (no secret-key crossing), profile section gains a circular preview + upload/replace/remove controls. Server-side sharp crop + resize + WebP conversion deferred; uploads stored as-is up to 2 MB.
- 2026-04-25: Added email change flow to `/account`; `requestEmailChangeAction` verifies current password through a non-persisting Supabase client then calls `supabase.auth.updateUser({ email })` so Supabase owns SMTP; `/auth/confirm?type=email_change` is now a branch of the existing route that calls the new `users.syncEmail` tRPC mutation to mirror the new address into our shadow `User.email` and emits `user.email-changed` (with `previousEmail` + `newEmail`), then force-signs-out and redirects to `/signin?emailChanged=1`. Profile section exposes a "Change email" inline form. Duplicate-email / same-email / bad-password branches surface i18n errors. `PendingEmailChange` schema kept for future admin flows but unused in the self-serve path.
- 2026-04-25: Added account deletion + 14-day grace to `/account`; `/account/delete` page lists what the flow does (soft-delete of memberships, anonymize contributions, audit retention, 14d cancel window) and requires typing the user's email to confirm, `users.requestAccountDeletion` stamps `deletedAt = now` and emits `user.deletion-requested` (with `deletionCompletesAt`), action signs the user out locally and redirects to `/signin?deletionScheduledAt=<iso>`. Danger-zone card on `/account` flips to an amber "Deletion scheduled" banner while `deletedAt` is set with a one-click "Cancel deletion" (`users.cancelAccountDeletion` clears the stamp + emits `user.deletion-canceled`). Hard-delete cron (anonymize + `supabase.auth.admin.deleteUser`) deferred to the ops scheduler pass.
- 2026-04-24: Added `/account` profile + security page as the post-sign-in landing (signin / signup / email-confirm / totp-verify all redirect here now). Sections: profile (display name blur-save, locale toggle that persists via `users.updateProfile` + updates the `NEXT_LOCALE` cookie, read-only email), password change (current-password verified through a non-persisting Supabase client, then `auth.updateUser` rotates the session cookie, emits `user.password-changed`), TOTP (full enroll / QR / recovery-codes / regenerate / disable flow, mirrors the dev overlay in a proper card), trusted devices (list + revoke). `users.updateProfile` tRPC mutation added (emits `user.profile-updated` with `changed` array), `auth.notifyPasswordChanged` mutation added so the web layer doesn't cross the tier boundary. Avatar upload, email change, and account deletion deferred.
- 2026-04-24: Added auth-totp MVP to `@monark/auth`; `TotpSecret` + `RecoveryCode` Prisma models with AES-256-GCM-encrypted secrets (`TOTP_ENCRYPTION_KEY` env var on api) and bcrypt-hashed 10-code recovery set, `otplib.authenticator` with ±1 window, `beginTotpEnrollment` / `confirmTotpEnrollment` / `verifyTotpCode` / `verifyRecoveryCode` / `regenerateRecoveryCodes` / `disableTotp` / `getTotpStatus` / `requiresTotpChallenge` / `markDeviceTotpVerified` helpers, `auth.totp.*` tRPC sub-router, `totp.enabled` / `totp.disabled` / `totp.recovery-code-used` events, `auth.totp-trust-devices` (default on) + `auth.totp-required-admin` (default off) feature flags. Sign-in flow now routes through `/signin/totp` when a challenge is required: `monark_totp_pending` cookie + middleware gate redirect all protected routes until the code is verified; trusted-device `totpVerifiedAt` is stamped on success so subsequent sign-ins on that device skip the challenge. Dev overlay "totp" panel dogfoods enroll / recovery-codes / disable. `/account/security/totp` settings page, admin soft/hard-wall enforcement, 5/min rate limit, admin reset escape hatch, and stale-enrollment cleanup deferred.
- 2026-04-24: Added auth-trusted-devices MVP to `@monark/auth`; `TrustedDevice` Prisma model (cookieHash stored, raw cookie never persisted), `recognizeOrRegister` + `listTrustedDevices` + `revokeTrustedDevice` helpers, `auth.trustedDevices.{mine, recognize, revoke}` tRPC sub-router, `monark_device_id` cookie (HTTP-only, SameSite=Lax, 400d) set from signin / signup / email-confirm flows, `ua-parser-js`-derived "Chrome on macOS"-style labels, `trusted-device.added` + `trusted-device.revoked` events, `auth.trusted-devices` feature flag (default on) gates the recognition path. Security page UI, new-device notification email, per-device Supabase session revocation, IP-to-country resolution, and TOTP-skip consumer deferred.
- 2026-04-24: Dev overlay gained a "trusted devices" panel with per-device first/last-seen timestamps, IP, and inline revoke.
- 2026-04-24: Moved all web server actions off direct `@monark/*/server` imports; added `auth.signUp` / `auth.notifySignedIn` / `auth.notifySignedOut` / `auth.markOwnEmailVerified` / `auth.requestConfirmationResend` tRPC mutations and a vanilla `createServerTrpcClient` helper for server-actions / route-handlers, restoring the service-tier boundary. Added `AppError` → `TRPCError` translation middleware on `publicProcedure` so clients can branch on `error.data.code`.
- 2026-04-24: Fixed sign-in / sign-out state not reflecting in UI after server-action redirects; `TrpcProvider` now subscribes to Supabase `onAuthStateChange` and invalidates the React Query cache so session-dependent queries (users.me, rbac, trusted-devices, current-org) refetch with the new token without a manual reload.
- 2026-04-24: Added email-verification to `@monark/auth`; `signUpUser` switched to Supabase's public `auth.signUp` so a confirmation email is emitted (Mailpit locally), new `/auth/confirm` route handler exchanges the OTP and flips `User.emailVerifiedAt`, `/signup/check-email` waiting page with resend button (5/hour per-user cap via `EmailResendAttempt`), `requireVerifiedEmail(ctx)` guard, `EmailVerifiedEvent` emitted on successful confirm. Password reset, invite bypass, and the `auth.require-verified-email` soft-gate flag deferred.
- 2026-04-24: Added password-strength rules to `@monark/auth`; `checkPasswordOffline` enforces length ≥ 12 + 3+ character classes + no email/display-name substrings, `checkPassword` adds HIBP k-anonymity (degrades open on upstream failure), signup form renders live per-rule checkmarks, `signUpUser` rejects before the Supabase admin call, new `auth.checkPassword` tRPC mutation for blur-time feedback. zxcvbn score + shadcn `PasswordInput` primitive deferred.
- 2026-04-24: Added auth-login-password MVP (`@monark/auth` + Supabase integration); `signUpUser` orchestrator with compensating auth/DB rollback, `signIn`/`signOut` server actions in web, `auth.ping` + `auth.session` tRPC queries, Supabase SSR cookie helpers + middleware for session refresh, api-side token verification populating `ctx.userId` and `ctx.activeOrganizationId` via `supabase.auth.getUser(token)`, `@monark/auth`-owned `UserSignedUpEvent`/`UserSignedInEvent`/`UserSignedOutEvent`/`PasswordChangedEvent`, minimal `/signup` and `/signin` pages. Password reset, email-verification UX, TOTP challenge, referral wiring, and full auth-aesthetics styling deferred.
- 2026-04-24: Dev overlay gained an "auth" actions panel (sign up / sign in links + sign-out button).
- 2026-04-24: Added rbac (`@monark/rbac`); canonical `Role` Prisma enum (MONARK_ADMIN, ADMIN, DEVELOPER, AMBASSADOR, STUDENT), `RoleAssignment` table, `PERMISSIONS` matrix + `Permission` type, read-interface (`hasRole`, `hasPermission`, `getUserRoles`, `primaryRole`, `isLastAdmin`), guards (`requireRole`, `requirePermission`), write path (`assignRole`, `revokeRole`) emitting `rbac.role-assigned` / `rbac.role-revoked`, three `rbac.*` tRPC queries (`myRoles`, `myPrimaryRole`, `myPermissions`). Migration also flipped `Invite.role` and `FeatureFlagOverride.role` from `String` to the enum. Admin role-management UI deferred until auth lands.
- 2026-04-24: Dev overlay gained an "rbac" panel pulling current roles + resolved permissions.
- 2026-04-24: Added organization-management MVP (`@monark/organizations`); `Organization`, `OrganizationMembership`, `Invite`, `OrgSlugRedirect` Prisma models + User back-refs, read-interface (`getById`, `getBySlug`, `getUserOrgs`, `getCurrentOrg`, `requireOrg`), `organizations.current` + `organizations.mine` tRPC queries, five lifecycle event types. CRUD, invites, switcher, and white-label deferred until auth + rbac land.
- 2026-04-24: Added `activeOrganizationId` to `TrpcContext`; null until auth wires the session claim.
- 2026-04-24: Dev overlay gained a "current org" panel pulling `trpc.organizations.current` + `trpc.organizations.mine`.
- 2026-04-24: Added user-management MVP (`@monark/users`); `User` + `PendingEmailChange` Prisma models, read-interface (`getById`, `getByIdOrThrow`, `getByEmail`, `getCurrent`), `users.me` tRPC query, `UserProfileUpdatedEvent` type. Profile-edit, admin ops, account deletion, and avatar upload deferred until auth + rbac land.
- 2026-04-24: Dev overlay gained a "current user" panel pulling `trpc.users.me`.
- 2026-04-23: Added feature flags (`@monark/feature-flags`); in-code flag definitions + DB overrides scoped by global/org/user/role, tRPC router, React hooks + `FlagsProvider`, audit event on flip.
- 2026-04-23: Added togglable dev overlay (Alt+D) with API health + feature-flag smoke-test panels; production-stripped.
- 2026-04-23: Switched the codebase to extensionless TypeScript relative imports (`moduleResolution: "Bundler"`), eliminating repeated Next-bundler breakage around `.js` suffixes.
- 2026-04-22: Phase 0 foundation shipped; pnpm + Turborepo monorepo, strict TS base config, Express 5 + tRPC 11 api, Next App Router web, module manifest with tier check, three codegen tools (`gen:module`, `gen:events`, `gen:routers`), CI workflow.
- 2026-04-21: Feature planning specs for Phases 0–3 under `docs/features-planning/` (auth family, RBAC, organizations, users, onboarding, referral, voting, contribution-estimation, auth-aesthetics).
